TL;DR: 提出ROADGS-T：一种基于自适应网格高斯表示的大规模道路表面重建框架，通过2D高斯surfel网格建模、道路结构感知自适应网格化和轨迹一致性引导的位姿鲁棒优化策略，实现高效高质的道路表面映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于网格的道路表面重建方法存在重建质量有限、优化成本高的问题，特别是在大规模驾驶场景中表现不佳。

Method: 1) 在网格上放置2D高斯surfel，显式存储颜色、语义和几何信息；2) 提出道路结构感知自适应网格策略，在车道线、路沿等复杂区域分配更密的surfel；3) 设计轨迹一致性引导的位姿鲁棒优化策略，从多个邻近位姿估计局部表面先验并进行自适应加权正则化。

Result: 相比传统网格表示和3D高斯原语，所提网格高斯表示更好匹配道路薄表面特性，显著减少冗余原语和重叠。

Conclusion: 提出的ROADGS-T框架能够在大规模驾驶场景中实现鲁棒且高效的道路表面映射。

Abstract: Road surface mapping plays a crucial role in autonomous driving, supporting high-definition map generation, lane-level perception, and automatic road annotation. Recent mesh-based road surface reconstruction methods have shown promising results, but they still suffer from limited reconstruction quality and high optimization cost, especially in large-scale driving scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose ROADGS-T, a robust and efficient large-scale road surface mapping framework based on adaptive meshgrid Gaussian representation. Specifically, we model the road surface by placing 2D Gaussian surfels on a meshgrid, where each surfel explicitly stores color, semantic, and geometric information. Compared with conventional mesh-based representations and 3D Gaussian primitives, the proposed meshgrid Gaussian representation better matches the thin-surface property of roads while significantly reducing redundant primitives and overlap during optimization. To further improve representation efficiency and structural fidelity, we introduce a road-structure-aware adaptive meshgrid strategy, which allocates denser Gaussian surfels to geometrically or semantically complex regions, such as lane markings, road boundaries, and height discontinuities, while maintaining a compact representation in flat road areas. Moreover, instead of relying on a single nearest vehicle pose, we design a trajectory-consistency-guided pose-robust refinement strategy, which estimates local surface priors from multiple neighboring poses and adaptively weights pose-guided height regularization according to their geometric consistency.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond Single Expert: Harmonizing Diverse Visual Priors in MLLMs for Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15054)
*Xiao Lin,Xiaohu Huang,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ViPS框架，通过融合多个基础视觉模型的互补空间先验知识，并引入高效先验代理和动态先验融合机制，显著提升了多模态大语言模型在空间理解任务上的表现，在多个复杂空间推理和3D理解基准上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常仅集成单一预训练基础模型来增强MLLM的空间感知能力，但不同基础模型提供的空间先验具有互补性，适用于不同任务。作者旨在充分挖掘多模型视觉先验的潜力，实现更优的空间理解。

Method: 1) 高效先验代理（Efficient Prior Proxy）：以最小推理开销生成多个基础模型的视觉先验。2) 动态先验融合（Dynamic Prior Fusion）：实现上下文感知的先验融合与注入，使多个先验和谐协作。

Result: 在多个复杂空间推理和3D空间理解基准上达到新的state-of-the-art性能，验证了多先验融合的有效性。

Conclusion: ViPS成功证明了融合多个互补的视觉先验能够显著增强MLLM的空间理解能力，为利用多基础模型提升多模态大模型的空间感知提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated substantial promise in spatial understanding. Existing works typically incorporate prior knowledge extracted from a pre-trained foundation model to further enhance the spatial awareness of MLLMs. In this paper, we first reveal that when integrating diverse foundation models into MLLMs, different models provide complementary spatial priors that benefit different tasks. Motivated by this, we propose $\textbf{ViPS}$, a novel multi-model prior framework designed to fully unleash the potential of incorporating multiple $\textbf{Vi}$sual $\textbf{P}$riors from diverse models into MLLMs for $\textbf{S}$patial understanding. Specifically, ViPS introduces an Efficient Prior Proxy to generate multiple foundational priors with minimal inference overhead, and a Dynamic Prior Fusion mechanism to achieve harmonious and context-aware prior fusion and injection from the prior proxies. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ViPS successfully harmonizes diverse visual priors, establishing new state-of-the-art performance across multiple complex spatial reasoning and 3D spatial understanding benchmarks. Project page: https://visual-ai.github.io/vips

</details>


### [157] [SUFLECA: Scaling Up Feature Learning for CAD-to-image Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15058)
*Saad Ejaz,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Javier Civera,Holger Voos,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SUFLECA是一个零样本CAD-图像对齐的弱监督框架，通过在大规模数据集上学习几何感知特征并采用几何一致匹配算法，在ScanNet25k基准上以更小计算量超越现有零样本方法，甚至首次超越了全监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本CAD-图像对齐方法依赖外观驱动的特征匹配，在遮挡或仿真到真实域迁移场景下性能严重退化，亟需兼具几何鲁棒性和跨域泛化能力的解决方案。

Method: (1) 在674K图像（12个真实及合成数据集）上利用归一化物体坐标（NOCs）进行弱监督几何特征学习；(2) 提出几何一致匹配算法，建立可靠的一对一CAD-图像对应关系。

Result: 在ScanNet25k上达到33.4%/42.3%的类别/实例准确率，比最强零样本基线提升10.3/12.2个百分点，且计算开销更小，并首次在该基准上超越全监督方法。

Conclusion: 通过大规模几何特征学习和几何一致匹配，SUFLECA在无需迭代位姿精化的条件下实现了准确、亚秒级的零样本CAD对齐，证明了弱监督几何学习在域迁移和遮挡场景下的有效性。

Abstract: CAD-to-image alignment aims to estimate an object's 9D pose (rotation, translation, and anisotropic scale) from a single RGB image, enabling applications in robotics and augmented reality. Recent zero-shot methods use visual foundation models to match image regions to CAD models, yet typically their correspondences are appearance-driven and degrade under occlusion or sim-to-real domain shift. To address these limitations, we introduce SUFLECA (Scaling Up Feature LEarning for CAD Alignment), a weakly-supervised framework for zero-shot CAD alignment with two key contributions. First, SUFLECA scales up geometry-grounded feature learning from pretrained visual representations through Normalized Object Coordinates (NOCs) supervision on 674K images spanning 12 real and synthetic datasets, learning compact geometry-aware features that generalize across domains. Second, we propose a geometrically consistent matching algorithm that establishes reliable one-to-one CAD-to-image correspondences. Together, these contributions enable accurate, sub-second alignment per object instance without iterative pose refinement. On ScanNet25k, SUFLECA achieves 33.4%/42.3% category/instance accuracy, outperforming, with a smaller computational footprint, the strongest zero-shot baseline by 10.3/12.2 percentage points and, for the first time on this benchmark, even surpassing fully supervised methods. Code is available at: https://github.com/snt-arg/SUFLECA

</details>


### [158] [Towards Hierarchical Structure Understanding of Newspaper Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15082)
*William Mocaër,Solène Tarride,Thomas Constum,Merveilles Agbeti-Messan,Tom Simon,Clément Chatelain,Stéphane Nicolas,Pierrick Tranouez,Sébastien Cretin,Thierry Paquet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对报纸图像结构理解这一挑战性任务，提出了两种互补方法：一是基于YOLO、LayoutReader和自定义算法构建的模块化自底向上流水线，二是名为Tiramisu的新型端到端Transformer架构，后者通过迭代分层机制显式建模文档层次结构。同时发布了用于评估历史报纸层次化信息检索的新数据集Finlam La Liberté。实验证明两种方法均能有效重建复杂报纸层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 报纸图像具有复杂的嵌套层次结构和密集异质布局，传统方法难以有效处理其结构理解问题，亟需能够显式建模文档层次的高效方法。

Method: 两种互补方法：(1) 模块化自底向上流水线，组合YOLO进行布局检测、LayoutReader预测阅读顺序、自定义算法进行文章分割；(2) Tiramisu，一种基于Transformer的端到端架构，通过迭代分层注意力机制同时执行章节与文章分离、块定位、语义分类和阅读顺序预测。

Result: 两种方法均能有效重建复杂报纸层次结构，对比分析揭示了各自在可扩展文档数字化方面的优势。

Conclusion: 模块化流水线灵活可解释，Tiramisu端到端方法通过显式层次建模实现高度并行化处理，二者互补有助于推进历史报纸的数字化理解。

Abstract: Understanding newspaper images remains a challenging task due to their complex, nested hierarchical structures and dense, heterogeneous layouts. In this paper, we explore two complementary approaches for newspaper structure understanding. First, we present a modular bottom-up pipeline that combines state-of-the-art open-source models: YOLO for layout detection, LayoutReader for reading order prediction, and a custom algorithm for article segmentation. This approach leverages existing robust components while maintaining flexibility and interpretability. Second, we introduce Tiramisu (Tiered Transformers for Hierarchical Structure Understanding), a novel end-to-end transformer-based architecture that explicitly models document hierarchy through an iterative tiered process. Tiramisu performs section and article separation, block localization, semantic categorization, and reading order prediction using highly parallelized attention mechanisms. Finally, we release Finlam La Liberté, a new dataset designed specifically for evaluating hierarchical information retrieval in historical newspapers. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of both approaches in reconstructing complex newspaper hierarchies, with comparative analysis highlighting their respective strengths for scalable document digitization. The Tiramisu training code, including the synthetic newspaper generator, is available at https://git.litislab.fr/tiramisu/tiramisu-newspaper-articles-extractor.

</details>


### [159] [Quantifying Training Membership Information in the Hyperspherical Embedding Geometry of Face Recognition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15084)
*Ünsal Öztürk,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文量化了人脸识别模型中成员身份与非成员身份在嵌入空间中的几何差异，发现训练身份数量是影响成员/非成员可分性的最主要因素，而骨干网络和损失函数影响较小。跨域非成员基准会放大成员信号，最后通过融合四个几何统计量可以进一步提升成员推断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于角度间隔损失训练的人脸识别模型，其训练目标仅针对训练集中的身份，非成员身份可能形成不同的几何聚类性质。本文旨在量化这种差异的幅度及其受训练时因素控制的程度。

Method: 在IResNet骨干大小、损失函数类型、训练时长和训练身份数量四个因子下，对180个人脸识别模型进行析因设计实验，计算四个基于聚类的几何统计量，并在9个基准上评估；最后使用分类器融合四个统计量。

Result: 训练身份数量对成员/非成员可分性影响最大，骨干网络和损失函数贡献较小；在同域参考上，成员信号随训练身份增加单调递减；跨域非成员基准会放大成员信号；融合四个统计量可揭示更多成员信息。

Conclusion: 训练身份数量是控制人脸识别模型中成员推断风险的关键因素，跨域评估会高估实际风险，融合多几何特征可提升成员推断能力。

Abstract: Face recognition models represent each face as an embedding vector on the unit hypersphere by clustering embeddings of the same identity while pushing different identities apart through angular-margin losses. Because these losses act only on training identities, non-member identities may form clusters with different geometric properties. In this paper, we quantify the magnitude of this difference and what training-time factors control it. We compute four statistics based on cluster geometry across 180 face recognition models in a factorial design over IResNet backbone size, loss head, training duration, and the number of training identities, and evaluate each configuration on nine benchmarks. Our results indicate that the number of training identities has the largest effect on member/non-member separability, while backbone and loss head contribute far less, and that, on a same-domain held-out reference, the geometric membership signal decreases monotonically as more identities are added to training. We provide an analysis of cross-domain (pose, age, quality, ethnicity) non-member benchmarks and report that these inflate the apparent membership signal. Finally, we fuse all four statistics with a learned classifier to reveal additional membership information beyond the best individual statistic.

</details>


### [160] [AlphaWiSE: Adaptive Weight Interpolation for Continual Multimodal Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15094)
*Sarthak Jain,Qiran Hu,Zhen Zhu,Yaoyao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AlphaWiSE方法，通过对两个冻结的源检查点进行权重空间插值（每个参数张量学习一个共享标量插值系数），实现了多模态持续学习中跨模态对齐的保持，在音频-图像-文本检索任务上优于现有持续学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型（如CLIP）在持续适应新数据时，跨模态对齐会被破坏；传统持续学习方法只返回单一检查点，无法为不同检索方向提供不同的稳定性-可塑性权衡。

Method: 提出AlphaWiSE，一种事后权重空间插值方法：组合两个冻结的源检查点，对每个参数张量（由检查点键标识）拟合一个标量插值系数（所有张量条目共享），该系数在小样本记忆上拟合，用于生成插值后的检查点。模型架构和参数量与源检查点相同，不增加推理时间。

Result: 在音频-图像-文本检索任务上，AlphaWiSE在多个检索方向和评估指标上一致优于强持续学习基线。

Conclusion: AlphaWiSE通过简单的权重空间插值有效解决了多模态持续学习中的跨模态对齐保持问题，且不增加推理开销，证明了该方法在多个检索方向上的普适有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal models such as CLIP learn a shared embedding space for cross-modal retrieval, but continual adaptation to sequentially arriving data can disrupt the cross-modal alignment acquired from earlier phases. Conventional continual-learning methods return a single checkpoint, which commits every retrieval direction to the same stability-plasticity trade-off. We propose AlphaWiSE, a post-hoc weight-space interpolation method that composes two frozen source checkpoints. For each aligned parameter tensor identified by its checkpoint key, AlphaWiSE fits one scalar interpolation coefficient shared by all tensor entries. The coefficients are fitted on a smaller exemplar memory and used to materialize one interpolated checkpoint. The deployed model has the same architecture and parameter count as either source checkpoint, which does not require additional inference time. Extensive experiments on audio-image-text retrieval show consistent improvements over strong continual-learning baselines across multiple retrieval directions and evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [161] [QuReC: All-in-One Image Restoration with Query-Specific Guidance and Local-Global Response Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15097)
*Shen Zhou,Jinghui Zhang,Wenbo Huang,Xuwei Qian,Zhen Wu,Guangwen Peng,Zhiyuan Li,Ding Ding,Dian Shen,Fang Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QuReC 提出了一种用于全能图像修复的统一框架，通过退化引导的查询重建模块（DQRM）实现空间自适应的退化感知表示，并结合局部-全局响应校准模块（LGRCM）增强特征聚合的可靠性，在多个基准上取得了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全能图像修复方法依赖图像级提示或共享引导，无法有效处理空间异质性退化或混合退化场景；且空间自适应引导不足以满足可靠聚合局部邻域与全局上下文信息的需求。

Method: 提出 QuReC 框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 退化引导查询重建模块（DQRM），将每个空间查询与退化原型空间匹配，重建查询特定的退化感知表示，并采用弱监督原型匹配学习策略提升优化稳定性与退化语义一致性；2) 局部-全局响应校准模块（LGRCM），通过双分支聚合局部与全局响应，并利用可学习先验校准聚合结果，增强细节建模与上下文建模的协调性。

Result: 在多个全能图像修复基准上，QuReC 取得了优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: QuReC 通过细粒度的空间自适应退化引导与局部-全局响应校准，有效解决了多退化类型下的图像修复问题，验证了所提框架的有效性。

Abstract: All-in-one image restoration aims to recover clean images degraded by multiple corruption types using a single unified model. Existing methods typically rely on image-level prompts or shared guidance to handle diverse degradations. However, such a paradigm becomes inadequate when degradations are spatially heterogeneous or even coexist in mixed forms within a single image. Yet spatially adaptive guidance alone is not sufficient, since accurate restoration also requires each spatial query to reliably aggregate complementary information from local neighborhoods and global contexts. To this end, we propose QuReC, a unified framework for all-in-one image restoration. QuReC consists of a Degradation-Guided Query Reconstruction Module (DQRM) and a Local-Global Response Calibration Module (LGRCM). Specifically, DQRM matches each spatial query against a degradation prototype space to reconstruct a query-specific degradation-aware representation, thereby providing fine-grained spatially adaptive restoration guidance. To further stabilize this query-wise matching process, we introduce a weakly supervised prototype matching learning strategy to improve optimization stability and degradation semantic consistency. Meanwhile, LGRCM performs local-global dual-branch aggregation and calibrates the aggregated responses with learnable priors, improving the reliability of feature aggregation and the coordination between local detail modeling and global context modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that QuReC achieves superior performance on multiple all-in-one image restoration benchmarks. The code is released at https://github.com/zhoushen1/QuReC.

</details>


### [162] [DAPGNet: Dynamic Adaptive Physics-Guided Graph Diffusion Network for Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15128)
*Pengkun Wang,Weijia Cao,Ning Wang,Xiaofei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAPGNet是一种动态自适应物理引导图扩散网络，通过注入结构约束的物理先验（连续光谱响应）到关系级图学习中，实现高光谱图像分类，在四个数据集上显著优于CNN、Transformer、Mamba和图基基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图分类器通常基于空间邻近性、超像素连通性或特征亲和力构建图拓扑，但连续波段携带的光谱物理先验对拓扑估计和消息传播的影响有限，亟需将光谱物理先验显式注入图学习过程。

Method: 1) 将连续光谱响应编码为节点级多尺度物理先验表示；2) 两阶段图构造器结合光谱-空间亲和力、物理先验一致性和空间距离，形成物理先验感知稀疏拓扑；3) 图扩散中将学习到的边权重转化为加性注意力偏置，物理门控在节点和特征维度上对图聚合特征与投影物理先验特征进行插值；4) 跨尺度融合整合不同扩散深度的节点状态；5) 联合优化主分类损失、辅助监督和二阶光谱平滑正则化。

Result: 在Indian Pines、WHU-Hi-LongKou、Houston2013和Houston2018四个数据集上，DAPGNet在OA、AA和Kappa指标上均超越CNN、Transformer、Mamba和图基基线，AA分别提升3.64~7.31个百分点。

Conclusion: 物理先验提取、先验感知拓扑构造、物理门控传播和光谱平滑正则化具有互补效应，共同提升高光谱图像分类性能。

Abstract: Hyperspectral image (HSI) classification requires reliable pixel-relation modeling under spectral variability, mixed pixels, and heterogeneous boundaries. Existing graph-based HSI classifiers usually construct graph topology from spatial proximity, superpixel connectivity, or learned feature affinity. However, the spectral physical prior carried by contiguous bands has limited influence on topology estimation and message propagation. This paper presents DAPGNet, a dynamic adaptive physics-guided graph diffusion network that injects a structure-constrained physical prior into relation-level graph learning. DAPGNet first encodes contiguous spectral responses into node-wise multiscale physical-prior representations. A two-stage graph constructor then combines spectral-spatial affinity, physical-prior consistency, and spatial distance to form a physical-prior-aware sparse topology. During graph diffusion, learned edge weights are transformed into additive attention biases, while a physical gate performs node-wise and feature-wise interpolation between graph-aggregated features and projected physical-prior features. Cross-scale fusion integrates node states from different diffusion depths, and the network is optimized with main classification, auxiliary supervision, and second-order spectral smoothness regularization. Experiments on Indian Pines, WHU-Hi-LongKou, Houston2013, and Houston2018 show that DAPGNet achieves the best OA, AA, and Kappa among representative CNN-, Transformer-, Mamba-, and graph-based baselines. It improves AA over the strongest competing method by 3.64 to 7.31 percentage points across the four datasets. Ablation and sensitivity analyses further support the complementary effects of physical-prior extraction, prior-aware topology construction, physics-gated propagation, and spectral smoothness regularization.

</details>


### [163] [Ray-based phase error correction for miniaturized DOE projector-based FPP under single-directional hyperbolic projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15139)
*Seung-Jae Son,Yatong An,Jae-Sang Hyun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对微型DOE投影仪FPP系统中的非线性投影伪影问题，提出了一种基于投影射线的相位误差校正框架，通过单方向双曲条纹估计投影仪针孔位置，无需立体标定即可恢复投影仪几何，并利用单标定位姿构建精化模型，显著提升了非线性投影条件下的重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型DOE投影仪的FPP系统因非线性投影特性和有限图案可控性，导致严重的相位伪影，现有方法依赖图像域处理或邻域像素，未能充分利用投影仪几何信息，校正效果受限。

Method: 1) 提出基于投影射线的相位误差校正框架，沿投影仪针孔发出的射线建模相位伪影。2) 提出基于单方向双曲条纹的投影仪针孔估计方法，无需立体标定即可恢复投影仪几何。3) 采用数据高效策略，仅需单标定位姿即可构建精化模型。

Result: 在微型DOE投影仪FPP系统上，非线性投影条件下重建精度显著提升，验证了方法的鲁棒性和物理一致性。

Conclusion: 所提基于射线几何的相位误差校正方法有效解决了微型DOE投影仪的非线性伪影问题，具有物理一致性和数据高效性，为FPP系统在实际部署中的精度提升提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Fringe Projection Profilometry (FPP) systems using miniaturized DOE pro-jectors often suffer from severe phase artifacts due to nonlinear projection characteristics and limited pattern controllability. We propose a ray-based phase error correction framework that models phase artifacts along projection rays from the projector pinhole, incorporating projector geometry without re-lying on image-domain processing or neighboring pixels. A projector pinhole estimation method based on a single-directional hyperbolic fringe pattern is introduced, through which projector geometry can be recovered without stereo calibration. In addition, a data-efficient strategy constructs the re-finement model from a single calibration pose. Experiments on miniaturized DOE projector-based FPP systems demonstrate significant improvements in reconstruction accuracy under nonlinear projection conditions, confirming the robustness and physical consistency of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [164] [MAGiSt3R: Multi-Agent Feed-forward 3D Reconstruction from Monocular RGB Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15211)
*Ziren Gong,Xiaohan Li,Fabio Tosi,Ninghui Xu,Stefano Mattoccia,Jianfei Cai,Matteo Poggi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAGiSt3R是一个多智能体3D重建框架，利用3R系列前馈模型和MAGMA合并模型，对单目RGB视频进行近10 FPS的实时重建和相机跟踪，并通过位姿图优化减少累积漂移，在合成和真实数据集上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D重建方法在处理单目RGB视频时速度慢且存在累积相机漂移问题，亟需一种既能实现高帧率实时重建又能保持高精度的解决方案。

Method: 采用3R系列前馈模型处理RGB视频回归局部点图，设计MAGMA合并模型在智能体内部和智能体间两个层级组合局部点图，并引入位姿图优化以缓解前馈流水线中的累积相机漂移。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，MAGiSt3R在重建精度和相机跟踪准确性方面均超越现有最先进方法，且运行速度接近10 FPS。

Conclusion: MAGiSt3R证明了多智能体架构结合前馈模型与优化策略能够有效平衡单目RGB视频3D重建的速度与精度，为实时高质量重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: This paper presents MAGiSt3R, a multi-agent 3D reconstruction framework performing reconstruction and camera tracking for monocular RGB videos at almost 10 FPS. MAGiSt3R relies on a feed-forward model from the 3R family to process RGB videos and regress local point maps, and on a merging model, MAGMA, that combines local maps at both intra-agent and inter-agent levels to obtain the final global point map. Furthermore, MAGiSt3R performs pose graph optimization to mitigate cumulative camera drift occurring along the feed-forward pipeline. We evaluate MAGiSt3R on both synthetic and real-world datasets, demonstrating its superior reconstruction and camera tracking accuracy compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [165] [Symbal: Detecting Systematic Misalignments in Model-Generated Captions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15216)
*Maya Varma,Jean-Benoit Delbrouck,Sophie Ostmeier,Akshay Chaudhari,Curtis Langlotz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出系统性错位检测任务，用于识别多模态大语言模型（MLLM）生成图像描述中的系统性错误；提出Symbal方法，利用现成基础模型在双阶段结构中检测此类错位，并构建包含170万图像-文本对和420个数据集的SymbalBench基准；Symbal在基准上准确识别63.8%数据集的系统性错位，性能较最优基线提升近4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM生成的图像描述中常存在系统性错位错误（即特定视觉特征与重复出现的描述错误强相关），目前缺乏自动检测此类错误的方法和评估基准。

Method: 提出Symbal，采用双阶段结构，利用现成基础模型（off-the-shelf foundation models）识别系统性错位并以自然语言总结结果；同时构建SymbalBench基准，覆盖自然图像和医学图像两个领域，包含170万图像-文本对和420个带标注的数据集。

Result: Symbal在SymbalBench上正确识别63.8%数据集的系统性错位，性能较最优基线提升近4倍；在真实世界评估中，Symbal能准确检测四种MLLM生成描述中的系统性错位，并可用于审计现成图像描述数据集。

Conclusion: 本文提出的新任务、方法和基准有助于用户在无需访问底层MLLM的情况下审计MLLM生成的描述并识别关键错误，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often introduce errors when generating image captions, resulting in misaligned image-text pairs. Our work focuses on a class of captioning errors that we refer to as systematic misalignments, where a recurring error in MLLM-generated captions is closely associated with the presence of a specific visual feature in the paired image. Given a vision-language dataset with MLLM-generated captions, our aim in this work is to detect such errors, a task we refer to as systematic misalignment detection. As our first key contribution, we present Symbal, which utilizes a structured, dual-stage setup with off-the-shelf foundation models to identify systematic misalignments and summarize results in natural language. As our second key contribution, we introduce SymbalBench, a benchmark designed to evaluate automated methods on our proposed task. SymbalBench consists of 1.7 million image-text pairs from two domains (natural and medical images), organized into 420 vision-language datasets with annotated systematic misalignments. Symbal exhibits strong performance on this benchmark, correctly identifying systematic misalignments in 63.8% of datasets, a nearly 4x improvement over the closest baseline. We supplement our evaluations on SymbalBench with real-world evaluations, showing that (1) Symbal can accurately surface systematic misalignments in captions generated by four MLLMs and (2) Symbal is a powerful tool for auditing off-the-shelf image-caption datasets. Ultimately, our novel task, method, and benchmark can aid users with auditing MLLM-generated captions and identifying critical errors, without requiring access to the underlying MLLM. Code is available at https://github.com/Stanford-AIMI/Symbal.

</details>


### [166] [Structural-Semantic Reciprocal Learning for Unsupervised Visible-Infrared Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15220)
*Moyao Tian,Shijia Liu,Yan Yang,Xin Yuan,Minshi Chen,Wei Wang,Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出结构-语义互学习框架（SSRL），通过细粒度结构解耦（FSD）提取身体部位空间锚点，并设计闭环语义校准（CSC）机制过滤伪标签噪声，将开环聚类关联转化为自校正闭环系统，在无监督可见光-红外行人重识别任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督跨模态行人重识别方法采用渐进式关联范式，但存在两个关键瓶颈：依赖模糊的全局特征表示，以及伪标签噪声在开环方式下不受抑制地传播，导致错误累积。

Method: 提出SSRL框架：(1) 细粒度结构解耦（FSD）提取判别性的身体部位基元作为可靠空间锚点；(2) 闭环语义校准（CSC）机制在每个epoch重构共享语义原型并反馈回训练循环，在下一聚类周期前过滤伪标签噪声；(3) 结构与语义学习的互交互实现鲁棒的跨模态表示。

Result: 在SYSU-MM01和RegDB数据集上超越现有无监督跨模态行人重识别方法，且在RegDB上超过若干有监督方法。

Conclusion: 结构-语义互学习能够有效解决开环关联中的错误传播问题，通过闭环自校正机制实现更鲁棒的跨模态表示学习。

Abstract: Unsupervised visible-infrared person re-identification (USVI-ReID) is challenging due to the large modality gap and the lack of cross-modal identity annotations. Progressive association paradigms have been proposed to gradually bridge the gap, but they suffer from two critical bottlenecks: reliance on ambiguous global representations and unchecked propagation of pseudo-label noise in an open-loop manner. To address these issues, we propose Structural-Semantic Reciprocal Learning (SSRL), a framework that transforms open-loop association into a self-correcting closed-loop system. Structurally, we introduce Fine-grained Structural Decoupling (FSD) to extract discriminative body-part primitives as reliable spatial anchors, complementing ambiguous holistic silhouettes with spatially consistent structural details. Semantically, we design a Closed-loop Semantic Calibration (CSC) mechanism that reconstructs shared semantic prototypes at each epoch and feeds them back into the training loop, effectively filtering pseudo-label noise before the next clustering cycle. Through the reciprocal interaction between structural and semantic learning, SSRL achieves robust cross-modal representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the competitive performance of SSRL against state-of-the-art USVI-ReID methods on both SYSU-MM01 and RegDB, notably surpassing several supervised counterparts on RegDB.

</details>


### [167] [Divergent Gaze Patterns in Artistic Viewing: Spatial and Temporal Signatures of Attention Across Autistic Individuals, Artists, and Neurotypical Observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15227)
*Mohammed Amine Kerkouri,Daphné Senggaran,Renaud Jusiak,Océane Lehmann,Marouane Tliba,Claire Wardak,Emmanuelle Houy-Durand,Shasha Morel-Kohlmeyer,Aladine Chetouani,Nadia Aguillon-Hernandez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过空间和时间两个维度，比较了自闭症成人、训练有素的艺术家和神经典型观察者在自由观看画作时的眼动模式。结果表明：艺术家与神经典型者在空间和时间上几无差异，而自闭症群体表现出独特的注意力特征——空间探索广度与艺术家相似，但时间模式不同（注视时间更短、扫描路径更不一致），整体更接近神经典型者而非艺术家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往自闭症眼动研究多聚焦于社会场景而非艺术作品，且仅关注注视位置（空间）而忽视了注视时序（时间）的影响。本研究旨在填补这一空白，系统地比较不同人群在审美体验中的视觉探索模式。

Method: 三组被试（ASD成人、训练有素的艺术家、神经典型对照）自由观看30幅画作各15秒。引入有向度量框架：空间维度上，用6种显著性指标（AUC-Judd、NSS、CC、SIM、KL、信息增益）比较一组注视密度图对另一组注视的预测能力；时间维度上，用MultiMatch、ScanMatch、FDISS和DTW比较个体扫描路径。所有注视点通过离散阈值算法统一提取。

Result: (i)艺术家与神经典型者在空间上高度相关（CC=0.96），时间上也是匹配度最高的扫描路径对；ASD则与两者均不同。(ii)ASD注意力存在分离：空间探索广度与艺术家相似（离散度、探索面积），但时间特征不同（注视时间更短、停留更少、扫描路径最不一致）。(iii)ASD注视在任何指标上都不特别像艺术家，而是略微更接近神经典型者。

Conclusion: 自闭症群体对艺术作品的观看是一种独特的、群体特异性的时空注意力特征，而非简单地偏向艺术家或神经典型模式。研究结果支持构建群体条件化的审美注意模型，并公开了所有分析代码和逐刺激结果。

Abstract: How different populations visually explore artworks bears on cognitive science and on accessibility design, yet most eye-tracking work in autism has used social scenes rather than art, and has analysed where the eyes land while ignoring when and in what order. We present a comparative free-viewing study across three groups, autistic adults (ASD), trained artists, and neurotypical observers, who each viewed 30 paintings for 15s. We introduce a directed, metric-grounded framework that compares groups along two complementary axes: a spatial axis, in which one group's fixation-density map predicts another's fixations under six saliency metrics (AUC-Judd, NSS, CC, SIM, KL, Information Gain); and a temporal axis, in which individual scanpaths are compared with MultiMatch, ScanMatch, a foveal-disc IoU score (FDISS), and dynamic time warping (DTW). Fixations are extracted uniformly for all groups with a dispersion-threshold algorithm. Three results converge. (i)Artists and neurotypicals are almost indistinguishable in both space (density-map correlation CC=0.96) and time (they form the most alignable scanpath pair), whereas ASD gaze diverges from both. (ii)ASD attention is dissociated: it matches artists' wide spatial exploration (dispersion, explored area) but carries a distinct temporal signature, shorter fixations, less dwell, and the most idiosyncratic (least self-consistent) scanpaths of any group. (iii)ASD gaze is not selectively artist-like on any metric; if anything it is marginally closer to neurotypical. Together these findings indicate that autistic viewing of art is a distinct, group-specific attentional profile in both space and time, and they motivate population-conditioned models of aesthetic attention. We release all analysis code and per-stimulus results.

</details>


### [168] [CRISP: Constrained Refinement via Iterative Squeezing Process for Robust Medical Image Segmentation under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15231)
*Yizhou Fang,Pujin Cheng,Yixiang Liu,Xiaoying Tang,Longxi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CRISP框架，基于“正区域秩稳定性”假设，通过无需目标域数据、无需测试时参数更新的源域唯一分割方法，利用概率排名稳定性推导鲁棒空间先验，在心脏MRI和CT肺血管分割任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像中的分布偏移是临床转化的核心瓶颈，现有域适应方法依赖预定义的模拟偏移或伪监督，难以应对真实世界中无穷且不可预测的分布偏移。

Method: 基于“正区域秩稳定性”假设，提出CRISP框架：通过潜特征扰动获得扰动不变的高精度（HP）核心和至少一次扰动下仍为前景的高召回（HR）支持区域，再通过迭代训练框架逐步将HP和HR区域压缩至最终分割结果。

Result: 在多中心心脏MRI和CT肺血管分割上，CRISP在HD95指标上分别降低0.14（7.0%提升）、1.90（13.1%提升）和8.39（38.9%提升）像素，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CRISP作为一种无需目标域数据、无需测试时参数更新的源域唯一分割框架，能有效应对真实世界中开放式的分布偏移，提升医学影像分割的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Distribution shift in medical imaging remains a central bottleneck for the clinical translation of medical AI. Failure to address it can lead to severe performance degradation in unseen environments and exacerbate health inequities. Existing methods for domain adaptation are inherently limited by exhausting predefined possibilities through simulated shifts or pseudo-supervision. Such strategies struggle in the open-ended and unpredictable real world, where distribution shifts are effectively infinite. To address this challenge, we adopt the "Rank Stability of Positive Regions" as a working assumption under distribution shift, and use it to derive robust spatial hints for source-only segmentation. Guided by this assumption, we propose CRISP, a model-agnostic framework that, unlike deployment-time adaptation, requires no test-time parameter updates and no target-domain data--a target-free, plug-in refinement framework that segments with frozen weights. Rather than using ranking to directly output masks, CRISP exploits the stability of probability rankings under distribution shift to derive robust spatial priors. Via latent feature perturbation, perturbation-invariant high-grade regions define a high-precision (HP) core, while voxels that remain potentially foreground under at least one perturbation define a high-recall (HR) support; these dual priors are then recursively refined under perturbation. We then design an iterative training framework that progressively squeezes HP and HR toward the final segmentation. Extensive evaluations on multi-center cardiac MRI and CT-based lung vessel segmentation demonstrate CRISP's superior robustness, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art methods with striking HD95 reductions of up to 0.14 (7.0% improvement), 1.90 (13.1% improvement), and 8.39 (38.9% improvement) pixels across multi-center, demographic, and modality shifts, respectively.

</details>


### [169] [ARMOR++: Agentic Orchestration of a Multi-Domain Primitive Set for Transferable Attacks on Deepfake Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15246)
*Christos Korgialas,Gabriel Lee Jun Rong,Dion Jia Xu Ho,Pai Chet Ng,Xiaoxiao Miao,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ARMOR++多智能体框架，结合视觉语言模型（Qwen2.5-VL）和大语言模型（Qwen3）协同五种攻击原语，实现对深度伪造检测器的高迁移性黑盒对抗攻击，在AADD-2025基准测试中显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在黑盒对抗迁移下可靠性显著下降，且现有迁移攻击缺乏语义感知能力，尤其在无查询约束条件下从CNN代理模型向Transformer目标模型迁移时效果不佳。

Method: ARMOR++框架包含：(1) Qwen2.5-VL视觉语言模型提供空间语义先验；(2) Qwen3大语言模型负责原语选择、自适应超参数重参数化和熵正则化扰动混合；(3) 集成五种互补攻击原语（密集优化、显著图方法、空间变换、频域扰动、块结构修改），协同攻击异构归纳偏置。

Result: 在AADD-2025基准上，ARMOR++在低质量和高质量图像场景下均显著优于现有基于智能体和非智能体的基线方法，盲目标攻击成功率（ASR）大幅超越当前最优的智能体基线，且在鲁棒防御配置下仍保持优势。

Conclusion: 当前深度伪造检测器的部署仍存在显著可靠性缺口，智能体协同编排是识别潜伏脆弱性的有效范式。

Abstract: The reliability of deepfake detectors frequently degrades under black-box adversarial transfer, as these models often rely on fragile, architecture-dependent forensic cues. Existing transfer attacks often lack semantic awareness and struggle to maintain effectiveness under strict no-query constraints, particularly when perturbations are transferred from convolutional surrogates to transformer-based targets. To address these limitations, this paper introduces ARMOR++, a robust multi-agent framework designed for high-transferability deepfake evasion. The framework leverages the Qwen2.5-VL Vision-Language Model (VLM) to supply spatial semantic priors, while the Qwen3 Large Language Model (LLM) orchestrates primitive selection, adaptive hyperparameter reparameterization, and entropy-regularized perturbation mixing. By integrating five complementary primitives, spanning dense optimization, saliency-based methods, spatial transformations, frequency-domain perturbations, and block-structured modifications, ARMOR++ effectively targets heterogeneous inductive biases. Rigorous evaluation on the AADD-2025 benchmark demonstrates that ARMOR++ significantly outperforms existing agentic and non-agentic baselines across both low- and high-quality image regimes. Statistical analysis confirms a substantial gain in blind-target Attack Success Rate (ASR) over the state-of-the-art agentic baseline, with further performance advantages evidenced against non-agentic benchmarks and under robust defensive configurations. These findings highlight a significant residual reliability gap in current deepfake detector deployments and demonstrate the efficacy of agentic orchestration in identifying latent vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [170] [HoloGeo: Mitigating Landmark Bias in Geo-localization via Evidence-Driven Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15255)
*Pengcheng Zhou,Xuanyu Liu,Yanchen Yin,Bobo Li,Shengqiong Wu,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉语言模型在图像地理定位中存在的 landmark bias（地标偏差）问题，设计了偏差度量指标 BI 和 BH，构建了基准数据集 LandmarkBias-3K，并提出基于证据驱动的推理框架 HoloGeo（配套高质量数据集 BF-30k），通过多维度奖励机制实现均衡注意力与联合推理，实验表明 HoloGeo 在保持原有性能的同时显著提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在地理定位中易受地标偏差影响，忽略地理线索或形成虚假关联，导致定位不准确，而目前缺乏对这一问题的系统量化与缓解方法。

Method: 1）设计 Bias Intensity (BI) 和 Bias Harmfulness (BH) 两个指标量化地标偏差；2）构建 LandmarkBias-3K 基准数据集；3）提出 HoloGeo 证据驱动推理框架，配套构建 BF-30k 结构化多证据无偏推理链数据集，结合多维度奖励机制促进均衡的视觉线索注意力和证据驱动的联合推理。

Result: HoloGeo 在 IM2GPS3K 和 YFCC4k 上保持优异性能，同时在 LandmarkBias-3K 上显著优于现有开源 VLM，验证了其鲁棒地理空间推理的有效性。

Conclusion: HoloGeo 通过证据驱动的推理框架有效缓解了地标偏差问题，为图像地理定位提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly improved image geo-localization, yet existing models remain susceptible to landmark bias, causing them to overlook geographical cues or form spurious correlations, ultimately resulting in inaccurate localization. To systematically investigate this issue, we first design two quantitative metrics, Bias Intensity (BI) and Bias Harmfulness (BH), to characterize the impact of landmarks exerted on model reasoning, and establish a comprehensive benchmark, LandmarkBias-3K. To mitigate landmark bias, we further propose an evidence-driven reasoning framework, HoloGeo, to improve the reliability of geo-localization. HoloGeo is supported by a high-quality dataset, BF-30k, annotated with structured multi-evidence bias-free reasoning chains. By incorporating multi-dimensional rewards, HoloGeo explicitly encourages balanced attention over diverse visual cues and achieves evidence-driven joint reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HoloGeo not only maintains excellent performance on IM2GPS3K and YFCC4k but also significantly outperforms existing open-source VLMs on LandmarkBias-3K, validating its effectiveness for robust geospatial reasoning.

</details>


### [171] [SceneBind: Binding What and Where Across Vision, Audio and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15265)
*Mingfei Chen,Zijun Cui,Ruoke Zhang,Hyeonggon Ryu,Eli Shlizerman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneBind 提出了一种全模态场景表示方法，通过结合全局语义嵌入和以物体为中心的语义-空间槽，同时捕捉场景的语义内容和3D空间结构，在跨模态场景检索和物体定位任务上达到SOTA，并支持零样本迁移到音视频定位等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态编码器擅长实例级语义理解（"有什么"），但缺乏显式的空间结构信息（"在哪里"），无法联合表示场景的语义和空间维度。

Method: 将每个场景表示为语义-空间实体：全局语义嵌入 + 以物体为中心的语义-空间槽（object-centric semantic-spatial slots），显式捕捉物体级语义、空间属性和不确定性；并提出SceneBind Matching方案，整合全局场景相似度与物体对齐，支持跨模态场景检索和物体定位。

Result: 在场景检索和空间检索任务上达到SOTA，并在音视频定位等下游任务上实现强零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: SceneBind兼容大规模预训练语义编码器，仅需少量额外token即可添加轻量空间建模，有效弥补了现有全模态表示在空间结构上的缺失。

Abstract: We present SceneBind, an omni-modal representation of realistic scenes with joint semantic and 3D spatial understanding across vision, audio and language. Existing omni-modal encoders excel at instance-level semantics (i.e., what is present), but often lack explicit spatial structure (i.e., where it is). SceneBind addresses this gap by representing each scene as a semantic-spatial entity, combining a global semantic embedding with object-centric semantic-spatial slots. This representation explicitly captures object-level semantics, spatial attributes, and uncertainty. We further propose SceneBind Matching, a semantic-spatial matching scheme that integrates global scene similarity with object alignment, supporting cross-modal scene retrieval and object grounding. To train and evaluate SceneBind, we curate a novel real-world binaural audio-visual dataset with structured semantic and spatial annotations, and propose a training protocol for aligning semantic and spatial signals across modalities. SceneBind is compatible with large-scale pretrained semantic encoders, adds lightweight spatial modeling with only a few additional tokens. It achieves state-of-the-art scene and spatial retrieval while enabling strong zero-shot transfer to downstream tasks such as audio-visual localization.

</details>


### [172] [Motion-Conditioned Multi-View Fusion for Myocardial Infarction Localization from Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15268)
*Guang Yang,Wentian Xu,Siyu Wang,Betty Raman,Lei Li,Vicente Grau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MCF-Net，一种运动引导的多视图融合框架，结合心肌运动线索与超声基础模型表示，在极稀疏监督下实现节段级心肌梗死定位，F1达72.4%，准确率84.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的心肌运动分析方法依赖手工特征或密集监督估计，标注成本高；基础模型虽提升了超声分析性能，但多数方法仅在单一视图上操作，且在心尖视图下节段级定位存在视图依赖歧义性问题。

Method: 使用EchoPrime基础模型提取双视图视觉特征；通过单张标注模板帧跨视频传递初始化点追踪，实现极稀疏运动监督；构建运动导出的节段感知软掩码作为粗略空间先验；设计运动条件融合机制整合双视图的运动与视觉信息。

Result: 在节段级心肌梗死定位任务上，MCF-Net达到72.4% F1分数和84.9%准确率，优于当前最优的运动-only、视觉-only及融合基线方法。

Conclusion: 融合心肌运动线索与基础模型表示，并在极稀疏监督下实现了可靠的多视图梗死定位，验证了运动引导融合策略在超声心肌分析中的有效性。

Abstract: Myocardial infarction (MI) remains a leading cause of mortality worldwide. Echocardiography (Echo) is a widely available modality for MI assessment, where regional wall motion abnormality is a key indicator. Prior learning based methods for myocardial motion analysis often use handcrafted descriptors or densely supervised estimation, but the need for extensive annotation limits applicability. Foundation models have recently improved vision-based Echo analysis; however, most methods operate on single views and segment-level localization remains unreliable under view-dependent ambiguity, especially in apical views. To address this, we propose MCF-Net, a novel motion-guided multi-view fusion framework that fuses myocardial motion cues with foundation model representations to localize infarction. Visual features are extracted using EchoPrime, a pretrained Echo foundation model shared across dual views. Cardiac motion is modeled with extremely sparse supervision: a single annotated template frame is transferred across videos to initialize point tracking, avoiding dense labels. Motion-derived segment-aware soft masks provide coarse spatial priors that selectively enhance features for challenging myocardial segments. A motion-conditioned fusion mechanism then integrates motion and vision across views, refining predictions without overriding strong appearance cues. On segment-level MI localization, MCF-Net achieves 72.4\% F1 and 84.9\% accuracy, outperforming state-of-the-art motion-only, vision-only, and fusion baselines.

</details>


### [173] [Online Neural Space Time Memory for Dynamic Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15271)
*Baback Elmieh,Lynn Tsai,Zeman Li,Srinivas Kaza,Tiancheng Sun,Gabor Csapo,Ali Behrouz,Yuan Deng,Stephen Lombardi,Steven M. Seitz,Xuan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实时动态场景新视角合成方法，通过解耦记忆更新与应用的频率（周期性更新、逐帧应用），并引入辅助记忆损失和记忆缓存机制，在保持实时性能的同时实现分钟级长时记忆，达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线多视角视频的新视角合成面临根本性权衡：需维持长时记忆以重建暂时遮挡区域，同时受严格实时约束。现有TTT模型虽提供强大记忆机制，但每帧梯度更新计算成本过高，阻碍实时应用且长上下文不稳定。

Method: (1) 解耦记忆更新与应用的频率：周期性执行记忆更新，逐帧应用记忆；(2) 跨视角注意力机制处理先前记忆状态与当前帧之间的形变；(3) 辅助记忆损失（Memory Loss）强制场景的持久内化；(4) 记忆缓存策略（Memory Caching）正则化活动权重，防止灾难性漂移。

Result: 在动态人体运动场景和分钟级在线记忆任务上实现了实时、最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过降低记忆更新频率、引入辅助损失和缓存策略，成功在实时约束下实现了长时动态场景记忆，解决了计算效率与记忆持久性之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Online novel view synthesis from multi-view streaming videos faces a fundamental trade-off: maintaining a persistent, long-horizon memory to reconstruct temporarily occluded regions while operating under strict real-time constraints. While Test-Time Training (TTT) offers a powerful memory mechanism, standard models mandate gradient-based memory updates at every frame to adapt to the changing motion in dynamic scenes. The computational cost of heavy memory updates precludes real-time application and can lead to instability over long contexts. Given that memory updates are more demanding than memory application and video content is largely redundant, we propose to decouple the frequencies of these two processes. Our approach performs periodic memory updates while applying the memory on a per-frame basis, using cross-view attention to manage deformations between the prior memory state and the current frame. To lock in the historical context, we introduce two critical mechanisms: an auxiliary Memory Loss that forces persistent internalization of the scene, and a Memory Caching strategy that regularizes active weights against catastrophic drift. Our method demonstrates real-time, state-of-the-art performance on scenes with dynamic human motion as well as minute-scale online memorization.

</details>


### [174] [MeanFlowNFT: Bringing Forward-Process RL to Average-Velocity Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15273)
*Yushi Huang,Xiangxin Zhou,Jun Zhang,Liefeng Bo,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MeanFlowNFT，将DiffusionNFT的强化学习框架拓展至MeanFlow生成器，通过构建瞬时速度预测器使得奖励优化适配平均速度采样，实现少步高效生成且保持严格策略改进保证，在图像和视频生成任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已成功用于对齐扩散/流模型与人类偏好，但现有方法如DiffusionNFT针对瞬时速度设计，无法直接应用于采用平均速度采样的MeanFlow生成器，亟需桥接这一差距。

Method: 利用MeanFlow恒等式连接平均速度与瞬时速度，构建诱导的瞬时速度预测器，将DiffusionNFT目标函数应用于该预测器，而采样仍基于平均速度以保留少步生成优势；并证明该方法具有严格策略改进保证。

Result: 在SD3.5-M上8项指标中6项超越现有最优少步RL调优方法；4步MeanFlowNFT在Wan 2.1上VBench得分84.33，超越50步LongCat-Video RL的82.57。

Conclusion: MeanFlowNFT成功将RL对齐扩展到MeanFlow生成器，兼具少步高效与严格理论保证，且实验性能显著优于基线及现有最先进方法。

Abstract: MeanFlow generators achieve fast few-step sampling by predicting average velocities over time intervals, making them attractive for efficient generation. Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a powerful way to align diffusion and flow models with human preferences and task-specific objectives. In particular, DiffusionNFT offers an efficient forward-process RL framework that does not require reverse-process trajectories or likelihood estimation. However, applying such RL methods to MeanFlow remains underexplored. DiffusionNFT optimizes instantaneous velocities, whereas MeanFlow samples with average velocities. To bridge this gap, we introduce MeanFlowNFT. Inspired by the MeanFlow identity, which bridges average and instantaneous velocities, we construct an induced instantaneous-velocity predictor. We apply the DiffusionNFT objective to this predictor, making reward optimization well-defined for MeanFlow. Sampling remains based on the average velocity, preserving MeanFlow's fast few-step generation. We further prove that MeanFlowNFT inherits DiffusionNFT's strict policy-improvement guarantee. Experiments on image and video generation show that MeanFlowNFT consistently improves baselines. Moreover, it outperforms prior state-of-the-art RL-tuned few-step generators on most metrics ($6$ of $8$ on SD3.5-M), and can even surpass multi-step RL-tuned diffusion while using only a few sampling steps. For instance, on Wan 2.1, $4$-step MeanFlowNFT reaches a VBench score of $84.33$, surpassing $50$-step LongCat-Video RL ($82.57$).

</details>


### [175] [Hierarchical Denoising For Multi-Step Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15278)
*Zezhong Qian,Xiaowei Chi,Chak-Wing Mak,Tianze Zhou,Ruibin Yuan,Yuhan Rui,Hengzhe Sun,Zhuoqun Wu,Yuming Li,Siyuan Qian,Sirui Han,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HDR提出了一种层级去噪框架，将视频潜在表示组织成树状结构，实现从粗到细的多步推理，在保持低延迟流式生成的同时，在六个复杂推理任务上显著优于现有方法，推理成功率从34.22提升至60.29（相对提升76.2%），推理速度比双向扩散快54.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频模型（流式自回归扩散和双向扩散）在复杂多步推理任务中难以兼顾逻辑一致性与低延迟流式生成：前者推理能力有限，后者推理成本高。因此需要一种既能进行全局规划又能保持高效流式生成的统一框架。

Method: HDR将视频潜在表示组织成树状层级结构：粗糙去噪层保留不确定性假设用于全局规划，精细层逐步精化为具体视觉状态。引入稀疏层级注意力模式（SHAP）降低时序注意力成本。整体实现从粗到细推理后再流式输出。

Result: 在六项多步推理基准上，HDR将成功率从34.22提升至60.29（相对增益76.2%），平均进度从76.00提升至89.56；推理延迟0.70秒/潜在，比双向扩散快54.2倍；仅用2%训练数据保留82.9%性能（双向扩散仅52.0%）。

Conclusion: HDR通过层级去噪框架有效统一了视频生成中的全局推理与流式效率，在复杂多步推理任务中展现出显著优势，并验证了在真实机器人物理交互与世界建模中的潜力。

Abstract: Video models are evolving into vision foundation models, yet they still lack human-like multi-step reasoning. Streaming autoregressive diffusion models are efficient but limited in reasoning, while bidirectional diffusion enables global revision with high inference costs due to dense frame-level denoising. Both paradigms struggle to achieve logical consistency and low-latency streaming for complex reasoning tasks. We propose HDR (Hierarchical Denoising for Visual Reasoning), a unified framework that integrates hierarchical latents into causal video generation for multi-step reasoning. HDR organizes video latents into a tree-structured hierarchy, enabling coarse-to-fine reasoning before streaming output. Coarse denoising layers preserve uncertain hypotheses for global planning, while finer layers progressively refine them into concrete visual states. A sparse hierarchical attention pattern (SHAP) further reduces temporal attention costs. We introduce a level-stratified multi-step video reasoning benchmark with out-of-distribution cases, covering six tasks: maze navigation, Tower of Hanoi, one-line drawing, sliding puzzle, Sokoban, and water pouring. Compared with streaming autoregressive diffusion baselines, HDR improves success from 34.22 to 60.29 (76.2% relative gain) and increases average progress from 76.00 to 89.56, demonstrating more consistent reasoning trajectories. HDR maintains low-latency streaming at 0.70 seconds per latent, achieving 54.2 times faster inference than bidirectional diffusion. It also retains 82.9% of full-data performance with only 2% training data, compared with 52.0% for bidirectional diffusion. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate HDR's potential for physical interaction and world modeling. Project demo: https://hierarchical-diffusion-reasoning.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Exploring Delay-based PUFs for Energy-Efficient Low-Overhead Security of Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14395)
*Venkata Prasanth Yanambaka,Uma Choppali,Saraju P. Mohanty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对物联网可穿戴设备的安全与隐私威胁，提出了一种基于物理不可克隆函数（PUF）的硬件辅助、低功耗、低开销安全解决方案。分析比较了Arbiter PUF和混合振荡器仲裁器（HOA）PUF两种架构，其中HOA PUF仅需2.7 μW即可生成加密密钥，相比Arbiter PUF的25 μW功耗更低，适合资源受限的IoT场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网可穿戴设备市场迅速增长，但这些设备持续存储和传输位置、心率等敏感个人信息，存在严重的隐私和安全威胁，因此亟需解决其安全漏洞。

Method: 提出硬件辅助的轻量级安全方案，分析并对比了两种PUF架构——Arbiter PUF和混合振荡器仲裁器（HOA）PUF在IoT系统中的集成效果。

Result: Arbiter PUF功耗为25 μW，而HOA PUF功耗仅为2.7 μW，两者均能以极低功耗生成加密密钥，实现鲁棒的安全保护。

Conclusion: 所提出的PUF架构（尤其是HOA PUF）能以极小的功耗开销为资源受限的IoT生态系统提供强大的安全保障，适合集成到可穿戴设备中。

Abstract: The Internet of Things (IoT) was introduced almost two decades ago. In the past two decades, technology has seen huge advancements. Many devices have become powerful and have less power consumption. Many IoT architectures and environments were introduced to help make life easier, especially in wearable devices. The market for these wearable devices has constantly increased over the years and is expected to reach its maximum in the next couple of years. They also pose a threat to users' privacy and security because they constantly store and transmit personal information such as location, heart rate, and other sensitive data. Therefore, addressing the security vulnerabilities is a crucial aspect of this research. This paper presents a hardware-assisted, energy-efficient, low-overhead security solution for wearable devices. Specifically, two Physical Unclonable Function (PUF) architectures: Arbiter PUF and Hybrid Oscillator Arbiter (HOA) PUF are analyzed for integration in IoT systems. The result shows that Arbiter PUF consumes 25 $μ$W, whereas HOA PUF consumes only 2.7 $μ$W to generate keys for cryptographic purposes. These architectures introduce minimal power overhead while providing robust security, making them well suited for resource-constrained IoT ecosystems.

</details>


### [177] [Better Privacy Guarantees for Larger Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14406)
*JacK Fitzsimons*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在计数依赖的零集中差分隐私（zCDP）框架下，隐私预算与组大小之间的最优关系。通过修正原始相对误差条件，证明了移位对数高斯机制能达到$v(n)=O_r(n^{-2})$的上界，并给出了$v(n)=\Omega_r(n^{-2})$的下界，确立了逆平方率为最优速率。在大计数和小误差极限下，主导系数介于$\pi/(4e^2)$和$1/\pi$之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pujol和Desfontaines提出了一个开放问题：在私有直方图中，是否可以对较大的计数允许更大的误差，并利用这种松弛来更强地保护较大组的成员。本文旨在对此问题进行严格的形式化研究和理论分析。

Method: (1) 对原始严格相对误差条件进行修正，引入边界容忍度$\mathbb{E}|\widehat{x}_i-x_i| < r\max\{x_i,1\}$；(2) 采用计数依赖的zCDP形式化隐私预算$v(n)$；(3) 利用已有的移位变换框架（shifted-transformation framework）构造移位对数高斯机制得到上界；(4) 通过信息论论证得到下界和主导系数范围。

Result: (1) 上界：移位对数高斯机制满足$v(n)=O_r(n^{-2})$；(2) 下界：对任意固定$0<r<1$，满足条件的机制必有$v(n)=\Omega_r(n^{-2})$，逆平方率最优；(3) 大计数小误差极限下主导系数在$\pi/(4e^2)$和$1/\pi$之间；(4) 当$r=1$时，零隐私损失的数据独立发布即可满足修正条件。

Conclusion: 在修正的相对误差框架下，计数依赖的zCDP中隐私预算与组大小的最优依赖关系为逆平方率，且该速率不可改进。同时给出了主导系数的紧致界限。

Abstract: Pujol and Desfontaines asked whether a private histogram can allow more error on larger counts and use that slack to protect members of larger groups more strongly. We study this question for fixed disjoint groups under add-or-remove-one adjacency. The privacy budget $v(n)$ depends on the affected count, is nonincreasing, and must bound both Rényi-divergence directions at every order. This is the count-dependent form of zero-concentrated differential privacy (zCDP) studied here. The original strict relative-error condition is impossible at count zero. We therefore make the boundary tolerance explicit by requiring $\mathbb{E}\lvert\widehat{x}_i-x_i\rvert < r\max\{x_i,1\}$, without changing the requirement at any positive count. Our main result determines the best dependence on group size. For the upper bound, we directly specialize an existing shifted-transformation framework. The resulting shifted-log Gaussian mechanism has a certified budget $v(n)=O_r(n^{-2})$. Conversely, for every fixed $0<r<1$, any mechanism satisfying the same positive-count utility requirement and count-dependent zCDP must have $v(n)=Ω_r(n^{-2})$. Thus the inverse-square rate is optimal under the repaired formulation. A many-count information argument further places the leading coefficient in the large-count-then-small-error limit between $π/(4e^2)$ and $1/π$, a factor below three. At $r=1$, a data-independent release meets the repaired criterion with zero privacy loss.

</details>


### [178] [A Measurement Study of AI-Environment Realism Gaps in Malware-Analysis Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14434)
*Zhiyong Sui,Lamine Noureddine,Mst Eshita Khatun,Sideeq Bello,Babangida Bappah,Justin Woodring,Aisha Ali-Gombe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究AI环境痕迹作为沙箱逃逸的新攻击面，提出AIprint探测框架，从284个开源AI项目中提取450个特有痕迹，评估7个沙箱后端与3个真实AI主机，发现AI环境痕迹可有效检测沙箱且实现伪造代价不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有沙箱逃逸研究聚焦虚拟机工件、时间差异等传统检测面，但环境感知恶意软件已能规避这些检测。随着AI软件生态普及，沙箱环境中普遍缺乏AI工具安装痕迹，形成新的可检测"真实性差距"。

Method: 构建AIprint探测框架：从GitHub 284个开源AI项目中系统性提取450个持久化痕迹（包括AI助手配置目录、模型缓存、环境变量、本地推理服务、包依赖等），编译为无特权Windows探测程序，在7个商业/开源沙箱后端和3个真实AI主机上评估。

Result: 传统VM检测无法可靠区分真实AI系统和现代沙箱；有12个AI环境痕迹在所有真实主机出现但在所有沙箱后端均缺失；214步安装实验证实AI工具安装与痕迹累积的因果关系；伪造实验揭示操作不对称性——复现逼真AI环境成本远高于检测浅层伪造。

Conclusion: AI环境痕迹构成沙箱逃逸的新有效检测面，且由于伪造成本不对称，防御方具有结构性优势。

Abstract: Sandboxing remains a core technique for observing suspicious program behavior, yet environment-aware malware increasingly suppresses execution when analysis is suspected. Prior generations of sandbox evasion focused on virtualization artifacts, timing discrepancies, and wear-and-tear realism. In this paper, we present the first systematic measurement study of AI-environment artifacts as a new sandbox-evasion surface. We operationalize this realism gap through AIprint, a probe framework that captures persistent artifacts left behind by AI-capable software ecosystems, including AI-assistant configuration directories, model caches, environment variables, local inference services, and package dependencies.
  We systematically extract 450 unique artifacts from 284 open-source AI projects on GitHub, compile them into unprivileged Windows probes, and evaluate them across seven commercial and open-source sandbox backends together with three AI-capable reference hosts. Our results show that traditional VM-detection baselines fail to reliably distinguish real AI-capable systems from modern sandboxes, whereas twelve AI-environment artifacts appear on the reference hosts and on none of the evaluated backends. A controlled 214-step installation experiment establishes a causal relationship between AI tool and package installation and measurable AI-environment artifact accumulation, while adaptive spoofing experiments reveal a fundamental operational asymmetry: reproducing convincing AI software environments is substantially more expensive than detecting shallow spoofing.

</details>


### [179] [Disclosure Divergence: Measuring Privacy Policy and Data Safety Misalignment at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14442)
*Mst Eshita Khatun,Lamine Noureddine,Sideeq Bello,Aisha Ali-Gombe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对6,051款Android应用的隐私政策与Google Play数据安全标签之间的一致性进行了大规模实证研究。使用基于LLM的提取框架和统一的数据类别模式，发现披露不一致现象普遍存在，尤其在敏感数据类别（如个人信息、设备标识符）和共享操作方面更为突出。研究提出了敏感性加权风险评分，揭示了持续监控和通信类应用存在更高的隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私政策和平台数据安全标签由开发者分别声明，可能存在不一致的表述，这给用户和监管机构带来不确定性。目前缺乏对这种跨渠道披露一致性的系统性大规模评估。

Method: 对6,051款Android应用，采用基于LLM的提取框架，依据包含14个Google Play数据类别和两种操作（收集与共享）的统一模式，逐应用、逐类别测量一致性，并引入敏感性加权风险评分。

Result: 发现不一致性在敏感数据类别（如个人信息、设备标识符）中尤为突出；共享披露的一致性低于收集披露；高风险集中在持续监控和通信类应用中。

Conclusion: 当前披露机制存在结构性缺陷，需要更强的验证机制和更高的平台级隐私报告透明度。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of mobile applications, user data privacy has become an increasing concern. While privacy policies describe how apps collect and share data, platforms such as Google Play provide Data Safety labels intended to summarize these practices. Because these disclosure channels are declared separately, they may present inconsistent representations of app data practices, creating uncertainty for users and regulators. In this work, we conducted a large-scale empirical study of disclosure consistency across 6,051 Android apps. Using an LLM-based extraction framework and a unified schema over 14 Google Play data categories and two operations (collection and sharing), we measure per-app and per-category consistency and introduce a sensitivity-weighted risk score that emphasizes high-risk data types. We find that misalignment disproportionately affects sensitive categories such as personal information and device identifiers, with sharing disclosures exhibiting lower consistency than collection disclosures. Elevated privac risk is concentrated in app categories associated with persistent monitoring and communication. Overall, our findings highlight structural gaps in current disclosure mechanisms and underscore the need for stronger verification and greater transparency in platform-level privacy reporting.

</details>


### [180] [Context Contamination in LLM Analysis of Network Security Logs: Poison with Passive Prompt Injection and Mitigation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14493)
*Rabimba Karanjai,Yang Lu,Hemanth Hegadehalli Madhavarao,Lei Xu,Weidong Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM在安全运营中心日志分析中面临被动提示注入攻击的漏洞，提出LogInject评估框架（含12,847条日志的基准测试），发现基线条件下攻击成功率高达88.2%，并引入跨条目碎片化技术Context Stitching（76.4%成功率），通过多层防御可降低90.4%攻击但仍有8.4%残留风险，表明LLM日志分析存在固有的混淆代理漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛部署于安全运营中心进行日志分析（包括摘要、告警分类和威胁调查），这些系统将外部日志作为自然语言上下文处理以生成安全洞察。这种架构模式引入了严重安全风险：攻击者可以将提示注入载荷嵌入日志生成字段中持久化存储，当分析人员查询LLM时载荷被执行。现有研究缺乏对这类被动提示注入攻击的系统化评估，因此需要建立系统性框架来评估这类威胁。

Method: 提出了LogInject评估框架，构建了包含12,847条日志条目（含2,569条对抗样本）的LogInject-1.0基准测试集，在三个生产级LLM上评估四种攻击目标（活动隐藏、误报生成、信息窃取、输出劫持）。引入Context Stitching技术，将载荷碎片化到多个日志条目中以绕过无状态过滤器并利用LLM长上下文推理能力，测试发现率达76.4%。同时评估了输入过滤、提示加固和输出验证相结合的分层防御策略。

Result: 基线条件下攻击成功率高达88.2%（模型间平均83.4%）。Context Stitching技术实现76.4%成功率。分层防御（输入过滤+提示加固+输出验证）实现90.4%攻击降低，但仍有8.4%残留漏洞。

Conclusion: LLM-based日志分析存在固有的混淆代理漏洞——不可信数据与可信指令在模型注意力机制中难以区分，需要深度防御架构和持续人工监督来保障安全关键决策。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly deployed in Security Operations Centers for log analysis tasks including summarization, alert triage, and threat investigation. These systems ingest logs from external-facing services and process network logs as natural language contexts to generate security insights. We demonstrate that this architectural pattern introduces a critical vulnerability: adversaries can embed prompt injection payloads in log-generating fields that persist in storage and are executed when analysts query the LLM, achieving what we term passive prompt injection. We present LogInject, a systematic framework for evaluating these threats. Using LogInject-1.0, a benchmark of 12,847 log entries including 2,569 adversarial samples, we evaluate three production LLMs across four attack objectives: activity concealment, false positive generation, information exfiltration, and output hijacking. Our findings reveal an up to 88.2% attack success rate (83.4% average across models) under the baseline conditions. We introduce Context Stitching, a novel technique that fragments payloads across multiple log entries to evade stateless filters while exploiting LLM long-context reasoning, achieving a 76.4% success rate. As mitigation, we evaluate layered defenses by combining input filtering, prompt hardening, and output validation, demonstrating a 90.4% attack reduction, although 8.4% residual vulnerability persists. Our results establish that LLM-based log analysis creates an inherent confused deputy vulnerability where untrusted data and trusted instructions compete indistinguishably for model attention, requiring defense in-depth architectures and continued human oversight for security-critical decisions.

</details>


### [181] [Fully Automated End-to-End Adversary Emulation from MITRE ATT\&CK Based Cyber Threat Intelligence Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14566)
*Jueon Choi,Seojun Lee,Sanggwon Yun,Kwanghoon Choi,Gunjin Cha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于LLM的完全自动化端到端对手模拟框架，能够从MITRE ATT&CK对齐的CTI报告中自动生成、执行Caldera剧本，并通过失败类型感知的恢复机制修正失败的Ability。在Claude Sonnet 4.5上取得最佳效果：每个剧本27.3个Ability，修正后执行成功率84.22%，CTI精确率73.95%、召回率52.48%、F1 60.50%。恢复机制在所有模型上持续提升执行成功率14.59%~17.23个百分点，在AURORA数据集上超越当前最先进的对手模拟系统AURORA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对手模拟方法要么执行预编写剧本，要么部分自动化剧本生成；最先进的AURORA系统虽能从CTI报告生成剧本，但仍需部分人工干预，且无法基于执行失败对剧本进行修订。这限制了对手模拟的自动化程度和实用性。

Method: 提出一个三阶段端到端框架：(1) 从CTI报告自动生成Caldera剧本（使用LLM解析报告并映射为ATT&CK技术/Ability）；(2) 自动执行生成的剧本；(3) 通过失败类型感知恢复机制，识别执行失败的类型并自动修正对应的Ability，重新执行直至成功。

Result: 在11份CTI报告上使用Claude Sonnet 4.5、GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Grok 4 Fast评估。Claude Sonnet 4.5最佳：27.3个Ability/剧本，修正后执行成功率84.22%，CTI Precision 73.95%、Recall 52.48%、F1 60.50%。失败恢复机制在全部模型上提升执行成功率14.59%~17.23个百分点。在AURORA数据集的10份报告上，最终执行成功率超越AURORA。

Conclusion: 所提出的端到端框架实现了完全自动化的对手模拟，无需人工干预即可完成剧本生成、执行和失败恢复。失败类型感知恢复机制有效且通用，在多个LLM上均显著提升执行成功率，并在与最先进系统AURORA的比较中取得了更优的执行成功率。

Abstract: This paper presents a fully automated end-to-end framework for adversary emulation from MITRE ATT&CK-aligned CTI reports using LLMs. Unlike prior work, which either executes prewritten playbooks or partially automates playbook generation, our framework unifies playbook generation, execution, and failure recovery in a single workflow. In particular, although AURORA, the most recent prior study, generates playbooks from CTI reports, it still requires partial manual intervention and does not revise playbooks based on execution failures. Our framework generates Caldera playbooks from CTI reports, executes them automatically, and revises failed Abilities through a failure-type-aware recovery mechanism. Evaluated on 11 CTI reports with Claude Sonnet 4.5, GPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Pro, and Grok 4 Fast, the framework achieved its best results with Claude Sonnet 4.5: 27.3 Abilities per playbook, 84.22% execution success after revision, and CTI Precision, Recall, and F1 of 73.95%, 52.48%, and 60.50%, respectively. The failure recovery mechanism consistently improved execution success across all evaluated LLM models by 14.59%p to 17.23%p. On the 10 CTI reports selected from AURORA's dataset, this mechanism further increased the final execution success rate, surpassing that of AURORA, which represents the state-of-the-art adversary emulation system.

</details>


### [182] [Qubes OS Security in the Public Record](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14587)
*Alfonso De Gregorio*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对Qubes OS的109份安全公告（2011-2025）进行协议驱动的纵向分析，发现其公开披露记录中79.8%的漏洞归因于上游组件（Xen、CPU/微架构等），而非Qubes核心逻辑。变化点分析显示2015Q1为关键断点，2018年后披露率呈统计平稳态势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Qubes OS的架构使得组件边界具有安全相关性，需要系统性地测量其安全公告记录，以理解上游依赖对安全态势的实际影响。

Method: 对109份QSB、464份XSA及次级漏洞事件敏感性序列进行协议驱动的纵向分析。采用确定性组件归因审计、变化点分析、过离散检验、严重性加权、删失敏感性分析、文档延迟下限估计，以及基线感知的漏洞发现模型评估。

Result: 464份XSA中有113份影响Qubes；109份QSB中87份（79.8%）归因于上游组件。变化点分析确定2015Q1为主要断点，2018年后年度披露率统计平稳。泊松推断在过离散诊断和负二项敏感性检验下稳定。S型VDM在短时预测中未显著优于滚动均值基线。

Conclusion: Qubes公开安全公告记录呈现稳定但非静默状态：披露活动在早期水平之上形成平台期，观测到的安全负担持续集中于上游信任锚点。

Abstract: Qubes OS is a revealing case for security measurement because its architecture makes component boundaries security-relevant. We present a protocol-driven longitudinal analysis of 109 public Qubes Security Bulletins (QSBs, 2011--2025), the official Qubes-maintained Xen Security Advisory (XSA) tracker, and a secondary vulnerability-event sensitivity series. The study measures the public advisory record rather than latent vulnerability incidence or realized compromise. The methodology combines audited deterministic component attribution, change-point analysis, overdispersion checks, severity-proxy weighting, censoring sensitivity, documentary latency lower bounds, and baseline-aware evaluation of vulnerability discovery models (VDMs).
  The results show persistent upstream dependence in that public record. On the official tracker, 113 of 464 XSAs affect Qubes; under primary labeling, 87 of 109 QSBs (79.8\%) are attributable to Xen, CPU/microarchitectural, or other upstream components rather than Qubes-core logic, with similar results under weighted views. Change-point analyses identify 2015Q1 as the dominant break in the quarterly advisory series, while post-2018 annual disclosure rates are statistically flat. Poisson inferences are stable under dispersion diagnostics and negative-binomial sensitivity checks. The attribution codebook performs well in a stratified 30-QSB audit, and S-shaped VDMs fit descriptively but do not significantly outperform a rolling-mean baseline in short-horizon forecasts.
  Overall, the Qubes public advisory record appears stable, but not quiet: disclosure activity plateaus at a higher level than in the earliest years, while the observed burden remains concentrated in upstream trust anchors.

</details>


### [183] [Bad Memory: Evaluating Prompt Injection Risks from Memory in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14611)
*Soham Gadgil,David Alexander,Sai Sunku,Franziska Roesner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于记忆的智能体系统中的提示注入攻击。通过沙盒合成工作空间，评估了Anthropic Claude Code和OpenAI Codex两个系统在四个模型上的表现。结果表明，虽然难以让智能体用不可信外部内容覆盖自身记忆文件，但已植入记忆中的载荷能成功攻击当前和未来会话。攻击成功率和载荷持久性因系统、模型、对抗目标和多会话攻击序列而异。持久性记忆改变了提示注入的威胁模型，需要在保护记忆更新的同时防止攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统通过记忆文件、行为偏好和知识库维持跨会话的持久状态，这虽然提升了智能体的实用性和自我改进能力，但也引入了新的攻击面——恶意指令可嵌入持久文件并影响未来行为。当前对这一新型威胁的研究尚不充分。

Method: 使用沙盒化合成工作空间，在Anthropic Claude Code和OpenAI Codex两个智能体系统上，针对Claude Haiku 4.5、Claude Opus 4.7、GPT-5.2和GPT-5.5四个模型进行提示注入攻击实验，评估攻击成功率和载荷持久性。

Result: 1) 让智能体用不可信外部内容覆盖自身记忆文件较为困难；2) 但已植入记忆文件中的载荷能成功攻击当前和未来会话；3) 攻击成功率和载荷持久性在不同系统、模型、对抗目标和多会话攻击序列下存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 持久性记忆改变了提示注入的威胁模型，需要设计既能保护记忆更新免受攻击、又不妨碍智能体有用自适应能力的防御机制。

Abstract: A growing class of agentic systems maintain persistent state across sessions through memory files, behavioral preferences, and knowledge bases. While this makes agents more useful and self-improving, it also creates a new attack surface for prompt injections in which malicious instructions can be embedded within persistent files and influence future behavior. In this work, we study prompt injection attacks in memory-based agentic systems using a sandboxed synthetic workspace. We evaluate two agentic systems, Anthropic Claude Code and OpenAI Codex, across four models: Claude Haiku 4.5, Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5.2, and GPT-5.5. Our results show that although it is difficult to make an agent overwrite its own memory files using untrusted external content, payloads already planted in those files can successfully attack current and future sessions. Attack success and payload persistence vary substantially across systems, models, adversarial goals, and multi-session attack sequences. These findings show that persistent memory changes the threat model for prompt injection and motivate defenses that protect memory updates without removing useful agent adaptation.

</details>


### [184] [MemPoison: Uncovering Persistent Memory Threats and Structural Blind Spots in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14651)
*Jifeng Gao,Kang Xia,Yi Zhang,Xiaobin Hong,Mingkai Lin,Xingshen Wei,Wenzhong Li,Sanglu Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MemPoison是一个针对持久化外部记忆系统安全漏洞的基准测试与分析框架，包含1227个手工验证案例，覆盖4种攻击类型、3种注入渠道和3种记忆基质。通过三级分类（L1直接单条破坏、L2组合多条破坏、L3上下文触发潜伏破坏）和机制性影响分解（MID）分析，发现写入时防御（如一致性检查）能有效抑制L1攻击但无法可靠防御L2和L3攻击，揭示了静态过滤的结构性盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化外部记忆虽然增强了智能体的连续性，但引入了持久性安全漏洞——对抗性内容可通过标准交互渠道注入、跨轮次保留并扭曲下游行为，现有防御机制对此问题缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 构建MemPoison基准框架，包含1227个手工验证案例，涵盖4种攻击类型、3种注入渠道、3种记忆基质，在7个开源和3个闭源模型家族上评估。提出三级分类（L1直接单条破坏、L2组合多条破坏、L3上下文触发潜伏破坏），并采用机制性影响分解（MID）分析防御盲区。

Result: 基线写入时防御（如一致性检查）能有效抑制直接L1攻击，但无法可靠防御L2和L3攻击。通过MID揭示了写入时防御的结构性盲区：看似良性的记录通过联合检索组合或触发条件激活后仍会造成危害。

Conclusion: 研究主张从静态过滤转向自适应、上下文敏感的记忆防御策略，以应对持久化记忆系统的安全挑战。

Abstract: Persistent external memory enhances agent continuity but introduces persistent security vulnerabilities: adversarial content can be injected via standard interaction channels, retained across turns, and later distort downstream behavior. To address this challenge, we propose MemPoison, a comprehensive benchmark and analysis framework featuring 1227 hand-validated cases across four attack types, three injection channels, and three representative memory substrates, evaluated on seven open-weight and three closed-weight model families. We introduce a three-tier taxonomy: (L1) direct single-record corruption, (L2) compositional multi-record corruption and (L3) context-triggered dormant corruption. Our evaluations reveal a distinct defense frontier: while baseline write-time defenses, such as consistency checks, substantially suppress direct L1 attacks, they fail to reliably suppress L2 and L3 attacks. Through mechanistic influence decomposition (MID), we demonstrate structural blind spots in write-time defenses, which admit seemingly benign records that later become harmful through joint retrieval composition or trigger-conditioned activation. Our findings advocate for shifting from static filtering to adaptive, context-sensitive memory defense strategies.

</details>


### [185] [Lattice-based extended withdrawability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14690)
*Ramses Fernandez-Valencia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Liu等人提出的可撤销签名方案，基于Fiat-Shamir with aborts范式构建了格基方案。将签名者特定值在明文中的泄露替换为基于索引承诺的隐藏机制，实现了可声明环签名：匿名性由黑盒使用的一对N签名提供，确认通过绑定签名与索引承诺打开实现。在量子随机预言机模型下，基于MLWE、MSIS、环签名的匿名性和基础签名的EUF-CMA安全给出了完备的安全性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可撤销签名方案在传输签名者特定值时存在泄露身份的风险，需要构造同时满足匿名性和可声明性的格基方案，以抵抗量子攻击。

Method: 将可撤销签名转化为可声明环签名：使用一对N签名作为黑盒提供匿名性（完全密钥暴露下仍匿名），签名者通过绑定签名和隐藏索引承诺的打开来声明身份。基础签名采用无hint、全t的Dilithium风格方案，一对N层采用格基一对多证明。

Result: 给出了正确性、可撤销性（即声明前匿名）、内部腐败下的不可伪造性和声明可靠性（claimability soundness）的完整证明，归约到决策MLWE、MSIS、一对N方案的匿名性以及基础签名的EUF-CMA安全。

Conclusion: 本文首次在格基密码学中实现了安全的可声明环签名（即扩展可撤销签名），解决了签名者身份泄露的问题，并提供了量子安全下的完整安全性证明。

Abstract: We extend the extended withdrawable signatures of Liu, Susilo and Baek to lattice-based constructions built on the Fiat-Shamir with aborts paradigm. Departing from an earlier draft that transported a per-signer shift in the clear, which leaks the signer, we realise extended withdrawable signatures as a claimable ring signature: signer ambiguity is provided by a one-out-of-N signature used as a black box (anonymity under full key exposure), and confirmation is the signer's claim, a binding signature together with the opening of a hiding index commitment bound into the transcript. No signer-derived value is published in the clear. We give complete proofs of correctness, extended withdrawability (as anonymity-until-claim), unforgeability under insider corruption, and claimability soundness, reducing to decisional MLWE (commitment hiding), MSIS (commitment binding), the anonymity of the one-out-of-$N$ scheme, and the EUF-CMA security of the base signature, in the (quantum) random-oracle model. We instantiate the base signature with a no-hint, full-$t$ Dilithium-style scheme and the one-out-of-$N$ layer with an established lattice one-out-of-many proof.

</details>


### [186] [FlowGuard: From Signals to Evidence for MCP Security Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14754)
*Baichao An,Pei Chen,Geng Hong,Yueyue Chen,Mengying Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FlowGuard是一个基于证据的MCP安全检测系统，通过结合语义风险分类、侦察引导的载荷精简、模式验证探针生成、证据裁决和历史引导精炼，实现对MCP交互中执行相关风险和语义风险的双重检测。在包含1,880个MCP案例的基准测试中，命令注入和文件系统访问的F1分数分别达到0.879和0.942，延迟比现有动态扫描器降低2.23倍，并在真实环境中从326个服务器报告了523个发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCP安全扫描器主要基于可疑语义信号进行推理，而非基于真实执行行为，导致风险评估不可靠（例如凭据类字符串可能只是占位符而非实际泄露）。需要运行时证据来验证执行相关风险，同时需要对元数据或返回内容中的语义风险进行精细分析。

Method: FlowGuard采用五阶段流水线：语义风险分类（Semantic Risk Triage）、侦察引导的载荷精简（Recon-guided Payload Narrowing）、模式验证探针生成（Schema-valid Probe Generation）、证据裁决（Evidence Adjudication）和历史引导精炼（History-guided Refinement），综合运行时证据和语义分析进行风险检测。

Result: 在1,880个MCP案例的基准测试中，命令注入F1=0.879，文件系统访问F1=0.942；端到端延迟比现有动态扫描器降低2.23倍；真实环境评估中从326个服务器报告523个发现。

Conclusion: 基于证据的检测方法能够有效评估MCP交互中的执行相关风险和语义风险，具有更高的准确性和效率。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) enables LLM agents to interact with external tools through metadata exchange, tool invocation, and response consumption. Existing MCP security scanners primarily reason about suspicious semantic signals rather than real execution behaviors, which can lead to unreliable risk assessment. For example, credential-like strings may simply be placeholders rather than actual leakage. This gap requires runtime evidence for execution-related risks and careful semantic analysis for risks carried in metadata or returned content. We present FlowGuard, an evidence-grounded MCP security detection system. FlowGuard combines semantic risk triage, recon-guided payload narrowing, schema-valid probe generation, evidence adjudication, and history-guided refinement. It verifies execution-related risks through runtime evidence and detects semantic risks in tool metadata and returned content. We evaluate FlowGuard on an executable benchmark containing 1,880 MCP cases across five vulnerability categories. FlowGuard achieves F1 scores of 0.879 and 0.942 on the execution-related Command Injection and File System Access categories, respectively. Compared with existing dynamic scanners, FlowGuard reduces end-to-end latency by up to 2.23x. In the real-world evaluation, FlowGuard reports 523 findings across 326 servers. These results show that evidence-grounded detection can assess both execution-related and semantic risks in MCP interactions.

</details>


### [187] [Is External Database Protection Static in Retrieval-Augmented Generation? Rethinking Privacy Preservation under Dynamic Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14811)
*Gang Zhang,Mingyu Tian,Xukun Luan,Yuanchi Ma,Jinyan Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种提示感知的动态层次差分隐私框架(PA-HDP)，针对检索增强生成中查询相关的动态隐私风险，实现细粒度的隐私保护与语义效用的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG隐私保护方法基于文档级静态风险假设，忽略了隐私风险与用户查询的强相关性，无法处理查询驱动的动态隐私泄露问题。

Method: PA-HDP包含两个核心步骤：(1) 提示感知的风险层次评估，动态判断不同查询下各文档的隐私风险等级；(2) 自适应的敏感实体替换与基于指数机制的文本选择，实现差异化隐私保护。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，PA-HDP显著降低了隐私泄露，同时保持了高检索质量，取得了比现有方法更优的隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 通过仅保护给定查询下真正敏感的内容，PA-HDP最小化了对检索语料库的不必要修改，验证了查询感知的动态隐私保护策略在RAG中的有效性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) enhances large language models via external document retrieval, but retrieved contexts may leak sensitive information. Current privacy protection methods typically rely on a document-level static risk assumption, treating all retrieved documents as having the same privacy leakage risk. However, this assumption overlooks a fundamental characteristic of RAG: the privacy risk of a document is highly dependent on the user's query, making privacy leakage inherently query-driven and dynamic. To address this challenge, we propose a Prompt-Aware Dynamic Hierarchical Differential Privacy framework (PA-HDP) for privacy-preserving RAG. PA-HDP first performs a prompt-aware risk hierarchy to dynamically assess privacy risks under different queries. It then applies adaptive sensitive entity replacement and exponential mechanism-based text selection to provide differentiated privacy protection while preserving semantic utility. By protecting only the content that is truly sensitive under a given query, PA-HDP minimizes unnecessary modifications to the retrieval corpus. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that PA-HDP significantly reduces privacy leakage while maintaining high retrieval quality, achieving a better privacy-utility trade-off than prior methods.

</details>


### [188] [The Distributed Open-Source Vulnerability Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14900)
*Peter Mandl,Paul Mandl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个概念框架，将软件漏洞管理描述为分布式信息交换与转换过程，分析了从漏洞信息创建、标准化、丰富化到上下文依赖解释的整个生态链，识别出异构信息源、身份与版本模型差异、时间变化和上下文依赖评估是导致不同扫描器结果不一致的主要原因，并讨论了其对结果解读、可复现评估方法设计及动态漏洞知识管理的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管漏洞信息基于共享标准，但不同漏洞扫描器对相同软件清单会报告不一致的结果，这种差异并非仅来自个别数据源或扫描器实现，而是在开源漏洞生态系统的多个阶段中产生，需要系统性地理解其根本原因。

Method: 本文构建了一个概念框架，追踪漏洞信息从创建、标准化、丰富化到上下文依赖解释的完整流转过程，系统性地分析了在生态链各阶段可能引发不一致的因素。

Result: 识别出四个导致扫描器结果不一致的主要原因：异构信息源、身份与版本模型差异、时间变化、以及上下文依赖评估。

Conclusion: 该框架对于解释分析结果、设计可复现的评估方法以及在实际中处理动态漏洞知识具有重要意义。

Abstract: Identifying known software vulnerabilities is a central task in software supply chain security management. Although publicly available vulnerability information is based on shared standards, different vulnerability scanners often report divergent results for identical software inventories. These differences do not arise solely from individual data sources or scanner implementations. They can emerge at several stages of the open-source vulnerability ecosystem. This paper presents a conceptual framework that describes vulnerability management as a distributed process of information exchange and transformation. It traces vulnerability information from its creation and standardization through enrichment to context-dependent interpretation. The analysis identifies heterogeneous information sources, divergent identity and version models, temporal change, and context-dependent assessment as major causes of inconsistent scanner findings. It then discusses the implications for interpreting analysis results, designing reproducible evaluation methods, and handling dynamic vulnerability knowledge in practice.

</details>


### [189] [A Queueing-Stability Criterion for Causal IPD-QIM Network Flow Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14954)
*Jiuxiang Cai,Guang Cheng,Guangjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对因果QIM网络流水印在突发流量下的稳定性问题，建立了反射排队模型，推导出缓冲区稳定的临界条件(μ_d > Δ/4或ρ < 1)，并通过仿真和真实IPD轨迹验证了该判据的有效性，为因果QIM水印的可嵌入稳定性提供了可测试的判据和量化步长配置基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多跳加密链路（如Tor、级联VPN）中，隧道化处理抹平了数据包长度和协议字段，使得包间延迟(IPD)成为主动流归因的主要载体。因果性要求嵌入器只能延迟数据包而不能提前，因此每次QIM对齐都会向延迟缓冲区注入非负驻留时间；无界驻留会破坏晶格对齐并导致宿主连接延迟不可接受。此前因果QIM水印能否在突发流量下稳定嵌入主要依赖经验配置而非理论分析，缺乏可测试的稳定性判据。

Method: 将嵌入器建模为反射驻留队列，采用固定双晶格等概率比特规则，注入过程是状态相关的（由当前间隔和比特决定）而非外生的。通过代换Y_i=δ_i-r_i得到代数Lindley形式恒等式，稳定性由大驻留量下的忙态漂移决定（有效间隔坍缩为零，平均注入变为Δ/4）。针对i.i.d.背景推导出稳定条件μ_d > Δ/4（即Δ < 4μ_d），针对平稳遍历和有限状态马尔可夫调制流量推导出时间平均强度ρ < 1。结合外生解码下限Δ ≥ cσ_ξ得到工作窗口Δ ∈ [cσ_ξ, 4μ_d)。

Result: 仿真确认了ρ=1处的尖锐相变仅由均值决定；在四条真实IPD轨迹上，每个模拟链限制在单一流中，该判据在流局部相关性和突发性下给出了正确的稳定性方向，而跨流池化均值会高估裕度。

Conclusion: 给出了因果QIM网络流水印的可测试稳定嵌入判据和量化步长配置基线。

Abstract: On multi-hop encrypted links such as Tor and cascaded VPNs, tunneling flattens packet lengths and protocol fields, leaving inter-packet delay (IPD) as the main carrier for active flow attribution. Causality lets the embedder delay packets but never advance them, so each quantization-index-modulation (QIM) alignment injects nonnegative dwell into a delay buffer; unbounded dwell breaks lattice alignment and delays the host connection unacceptably. Whether a causal QIM watermark embeds stably on bursty traffic has largely been left to empirical configuration rather than analysis. We model the embedder as a reflected dwell queue under the fixed dual-lattice, equiprobable-bit rule, where injection is state-dependent -- set by the current interval and bit -- rather than exogenous. The substitution $Y_i=δ_i-r_i$ gives only an algebraic Lindley-form identity; stability is governed by the busy-state drift at large dwell, where the effective interval collapses to zero and the mean injection becomes $Δ/4$. Away from the critical boundary, the buffer is stable iff $μ_d>Δ/4$ (i.e. $Δ<4μ_d$) for i.i.d. backgrounds, and, under stationary-ergodic and finite-state Markov-modulated traffic with instantaneous overload, iff the time-average intensity $\barρ<1$. With the exogenous decoding floor $Δ\ge cσ_ξ$ ($c=4Q^{-1}(ε/2)$), this yields the operating window $Δ\in[cσ_ξ,4\barμ_d)$. Simulations confirm a sharp transition at $ρ=1$ set only by the mean; on four real IPD traces, with each simulated chain confined to a single flow, the criterion gives the correct stability direction under flow-local correlation and burstiness, while pooled cross-flow means overestimate the margin.
