These results give a testable stable-embeddability criterion and a quantization-step configuration baseline for causal QIM network flow watermarking.

</details>


### [190] [NFSA: Non-Forward Secure Aggregation with One Server via Two Layer Secret Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15052)
*Yufei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于两层秘密共享的联邦学习安全聚合协议，通过结合Shamir秘密共享与基于PRF的2-out-of-2加法秘密共享，消除了用户间的直接通信和服务器的数据转发需求。进一步利用KhPRF支持高维数据聚合，并基于中国剩余定理设计了新的编码方法以减少用户开销。实验表明，在100个用户时通信效率提升近100倍，计算开销降低约17%，用户计算时间可减少51%-75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于服务器的安全聚合协议需要服务器在用户之间转发敏感数据，导致通信开销增加并引入潜在安全风险。本文旨在消除用户间直接通信和服务器的数据转发需求，同时降低用户端开销。

Method: 提出两层秘密共享协议：第一层使用Shamir秘密共享，第二层使用基于PRF的2-out-of-2加法秘密共享，消除用户间直接通信。采用KhPRF支持高维数据聚合，实现单服务器单次安全聚合。基于中国剩余定理设计编码方法，减少KhPRF调用次数并缓解模型更新掩码后的扩展问题。

Result: 100个用户时通信效率提升近100倍，计算开销降低约17%；输入长度为2^18时用户计算时间减少51%-75%。

Conclusion: 所提协议在辅助节点开销方面显著优于现有方法，有效降低了通信与计算开销，适用于联邦学习中的安全聚合场景。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training while preserving privacy by keeping data local. However, the risk of sensitive data leakage through model updates necessitates the use of secure aggregation protocols. Existing server-based secure aggregation protocols typically require the server to forward sensitive data shared between users, which increases communication overhead and introduces potential security risks. In this work, we propose a novel secure aggregation protocol based on two-layer secret sharing to address these issues. By combining Shamir's Secret Sharing with 2-out-of-2 additive secret sharing using a Pseudo-Random Function (PRF), our protocol eliminates direct communication between users, thereby removing the need for the server to forward data. We further extend the protocol with Key-homomorphic PRF (KhPRF) to support high-dimensional data aggregation and apply it to FL, enabling one-shot secure aggregation with a single server and no intermediary data forwarding. To reduce user overhead, we design a new encoding method based on the Chinese Remainder Theorem for the almost KhPRF-based mask, reducing the number of KhPRF calls and mitigating the model update expansion issue after masking. Experimental results show that our scheme significantly outperforms existing methods in terms of auxiliary node overhead. For instance, when the number of users is 100, our scheme improves communication efficiency by nearly 100 times and reduces computational overhead by approximately 17\%. Moreover, user computation time can be reduced by 51\% to 75\% when the input length is $2^{18}$.

</details>


### [191] [DataShield: Uncovering Risky Fine-Tuning Data Across LLMs Through Consensus Subspace Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15081)
*Zefeng Wu,Weiwei Qi,Jielong Chen,Tianhang Zheng,Di Hong,Chaochao Lu,Liang He,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DataShield框架，通过多安全对齐LLM的共识子空间对齐来识别高风险微调样本和响应片段，实现样本级过滤和细粒度片段级掩码，在保持下游性能的同时显著降低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖单一模型及其分词器计算的均值向量表示安全方向，存在有效性和可迁移性受限的问题。

Method: 基于多个安全对齐LLM的联合安全关键语义空间，通过语义谱分解提取共识安全与非安全子空间，然后衡量数据样本/片段与这两个子空间的相对对齐程度来评估风险，支持样本级过滤和片段级掩码。

Result: 相比SOTA过滤和掩码基线，DataShield在样本过滤上降低ASR 14.6%，在片段掩码上降低ASR 32.3%，同时保持下游效用且无需目标模型特定的风险计算。

Conclusion: DataShield通过多模型共识子空间对齐有效提升了安全风险评估的准确性和可迁移性，细粒度片段掩码比样本过滤具有更优的安全-效用权衡。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) on domain-specific datasets has become a standard paradigm for adapting LLMs to specialized applications. However, recent work has shown that even fine-tuning on benign task-specific data can substantially weaken the safety capabilities of LLMs. While existing efforts have made progress in identifying data responsible for safety degradation, they usually rely on a single mean vector computed over a specific model with its tokenizer to represent the safety direction, which limits both the effectiveness and transferability of their risk assessment measures. To address these limitations, we propose DataShield, a data assessment framework that identifies risky fine-tuning samples and response segments through consensus subspace alignment over joint safety-critical semantic spaces derived from multiple safety-aligned LLMs. Within these spaces, DataShield extracts consensus safe and unsafe subspaces using semantic spectral decomposition over safe and unsafe data representations. The risk of a data sample or segment is then estimated by measuring its relative alignment with the unsafe and safe subspaces, enabling both sample-level filtering and fine-grained segment-level masking. Compared with state-of-the-art filtering and masking baselines, DataShield reduces ASR by 14.6\% with sample filtering and 32.3\% with segment masking, while preserving downstream utility and avoiding target-model-specific risk computation.

</details>


### [192] [Automated Template-free Synthesis of Instruction-Centric Leakage Contracts for Black-Box CPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15118)
*Elvira Moreno,Tiziano Marinaro,Ryan Williams,Marco Patrignani,Roberto Guanciale,Hamed Nemati,Marco Guarnieri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化提取CPU指令级泄漏契约的方法，并实现了首个无模板工具malcos，能够在x86和ARM黑盒CPU上合成精确且可靠的泄漏契约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侧信道攻击利用CPU微架构行为差异泄露敏感信息，现有泄漏契约虽能提供安全抽象，但因微架构复杂导致手动提取成本极高，现代CPU缺乏专用泄漏契约。

Method: 提出一种以指令为中心的泄漏契约提取方法，实现最小人工干预；开发了malcos工具，作为首个无模板自动化工具，可在黑盒CPU上合成泄漏契约。

Result: 在x86和ARM CPU上评估，malcos合成的契约在合成过程中观察到的所有泄漏方面是精确且可靠的。

Conclusion: 实验证明从黑盒CPU中学习泄漏契约是可行的，为自动化构建CPU安全抽象奠定了基础。

Abstract: Side-channel attacks pose a significant security threat for modern computing platforms, because they exploit subtle discrepancies in CPU behaviors to leak sensitive information. To model the information leaked by a CPU via microarchitectural side-channels, recent work proposed leakage contracts: an ISA-level security abstraction that provides the foundations for secure CPU programming. Unfortunately, due to the complexity of current microarchitectures, devising a leakage contract for a CPU requires extensive manual effort and thus modern CPUs lack dedicated leakage contracts.
  We present a methodology to extract instruction-centric leakage contracts for major CPU architectures with minimal manual intervention. We implemented this technique in malcos, the first template-free tool that automates the synthesis of leakage contracts for black-box CPUs. We evaluate malcos on x86 and ARM CPUs, and show that the contracts it synthesizes are precise and sound with respect to all leaks observed during synthesis. Our results demonstrate that learning leakage contracts from black-box CPUs is feasible.

</details>


### [193] [Setup Complete, Now You Are Compromised: Weaponizing Setup Instructions Against AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15143)
*Aadesh Bagmar,Pushkar Saraf*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AI编码代理在根据文档设置项目时，会直接安装文档中列出的依赖项，而不验证其名称、来源或已知漏洞。攻击者只需编辑README、requirements文件或Makefile，就能将代理重定向到不可信注册表、已知漏洞版本或名称相似但错误的包，使文档成为代码执行向量。本文首次系统评估了通过普通项目设置文档进行的包安装时供应链攻击，涉及五种攻击类别、十二种场景，测试了前沿模型。研究表明，安装时安全性取决于代理-模型组合而非模型本身。来源攻击（如注册表重定向）几乎在所有场景中被忽视。确定性预安装检查可弥补大部分安全缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理在项目设置阶段会盲目信任文档中的依赖项信息，存在严重安全隐患。攻击者可以通过篡改文档实施供应链攻击，但目前缺乏对这一攻击面的系统性评估。

Method: 在五种攻击类别（包括错别字欺诈、分隔符混淆、注册表重定向、已知漏洞版本等）下构造十二种场景，基于真实安全事件，对前沿AI模型的编码代理框架进行系统性评估。

Result: 代理对明显的错别字欺诈有较好识别能力，但对分隔符混淆（如azurecore冒充azure-core）易漏检；来源攻击几乎全部被忽略；不同代理-模型组合表现差异显著，同一模型在不同框架上反应不同；安全提示只能改善被提及的维度。

Conclusion: 确定性预安装检查（在代码运行前验证名称、来源和版本）可以弥补大部分安全漏洞，是目前最有效的防御手段。

Abstract: AI coding agents set up projects by reading documentation and installing the dependencies it lists, without verifying their names, sources, or known vulnerabilities. By editing only a README, requirements file, or Makefile, an attacker can redirect the agent to an untrusted registry, a known-vulnerable version, or a wrong-but-plausible name: documentation becomes a vector for code execution. We present the first systematic evaluation of package-install-time supply-chain attacks delivered through ordinary project-setup documentation across production coding-agent harnesses, probing frontier models on twelve scenarios in five attack classes, grounded in documented incidents. The same model catches an attack through one harness and installs it through another: install-time security rests on the harness-model combination, not the model alone. Agents catch blatant typosquats reliably, but plausible separator-confusion names (azurecore for azure-core) slip through, and how often depends on the harness-model pairing. Source-based attacks like registry redirection are missed almost everywhere. The source blind spot recurs on npm and Cargo, where nearly every model installs the untrusted dependency; name detection carries over less consistently across ecosystems. Security-oriented prompts recover part of the gap but only for the dimension they name; a deterministic pre-install check that verifies names, sources, and versions before any code runs closes most of it.

</details>


### [194] [Beyond Success Rate: Cost-Aware Evaluation of Offensive and Defensive Security Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15263)
*Paul Kassianik,Blaine Nelson,Yaron Singer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出在评估安全Agent时，除了关注峰值攻击能力外，还应考虑成本-成功率维度。通过在攻防任务（Cybench和Splunk BOTS v1）上对比模型在固定成本下的表现，发现进攻任务（CTF）随测试时计算增加而提升，且开源模型可接近闭源前沿系统；而防守任务（SOC调查）更依赖于工具使用、遥测导航等操作技能，而非单纯的推理预算。建议安全Agent基准测试应同时衡量经济效率和操作适配性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全Agent评估通常只关注峰值攻击能力（漏洞发现、利用开发、渗透测试等），忽略了实际运营安全中每一步推理、工具调用、查询和富化都会消耗预算。因此需要从成本-成功率的角度来更全面地评估模型的实际可用性。

Method: 在攻击性Cybench挑战和防御性Splunk BOTS v1调查任务上，对语言模型安全Agent进行成本-成功率分析。比较模型在固定成本水平下的表现，并将性能分解为推理花费和工具花费两个维度。

Result: 进攻性CTF任务性能随测试时计算增加而提升，开源权重模型可接近前沿专有系统且保持成本竞争力。防御性SOC调查任务不呈现相同扩展规律：成功更依赖工具使用、遥测导航和选择性富化，而非单纯推理预算。

Conclusion: 安全Agent基准测试应同时衡量经济效率和操作适配性，而不仅仅是任务成功率。成本感知的、SOC原生的评估能更清晰地反映哪些模型在实践中有用，以及防御型Agent仍需要在哪些方面改进。

Abstract: Security-agent evaluations commonly measure peak offensive capability under generous inference budgets, emphasizing vulnerability discovery, exploit development, penetration testing, and CTF completion. Such measurements are useful but incomplete: in operational security, every reasoning step, tool call, telemetry query, and enrichment request consumes budget. We evaluate language-model security agents through this cost-success lens on offensive Cybench challenges and defensive Splunk BOTS v1 investigation challenges. Instead of reporting only best-case success, we compare models at fixed cost levels and decompose performance by inference spend and tool spend. Our results show distinct scalingregimes for red- and blue-team tasks. Offensive CTF performance improves with additional test-time compute, and scaled open-weight models can approach frontier proprietary systems while remaining cost-competitive. Defensive SOC investigation does not scale in the same way: success depends more heavily on disciplined tool use, telemetry navigation, and selective enrichment than on raw reasoning budget alone. We argue that security-agent benchmarks should measure economic efficiency and operational fit alongside task success. Cost-aware, SOC-native evaluations provide a clearer picture of which models are practically useful today and where defensive agents still need to improve. We present an interactive website with our results https://evals.frontier.security.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Generalized Neural Distributional Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14122)
*Natan Hilario da Silva,Vicente Garibay Cancho,Adriano Kamimura Suzuki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了广义神经分布回归（GNDR）框架，将深度神经网络嵌入经典概率分布参数空间，通过两步半参数估计解决深度架构非可识别性与最大似然理论的冲突，实现基于Fisher信息矩阵的不确定性量化，并在多种数据模态上验证了优越的分布校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在分布回归中存在两个核心矛盾：一是深度架构固有的非可识别性（参数冗余）与经典最大似然估计理论相冲突；二是缺乏严格的统计推断工具，难以对预测结果进行不确定性量化。现有方法要么牺牲神经网络的表达能力，要么放弃严谨的统计推断。

Method: 采用两步半参数估计策略：第一步将深度网络视为固定的非线性基函数展开，仅训练终端预测头；第二步据此提取解析形式的Fisher信息矩阵，利用多元Delta方法生成观测级别的置信带和容差区间。

Result: 在过离散临床计数数据、右删失转录组生存数据（混合治愈模型）以及从无结构人脸图像中提取的零截断年龄分布等多样化数据模态上，展示了框架的通用性和优越的分布校准性能。

Conclusion: GNDR框架成功弥合了深度学习的表达力与经典统计推断严谨性之间的鸿沟，为高维、复杂数据结构下的概率预测与不确定性量化提供了统一的解决方案，并已开源实现为Python包thetaflow。

Abstract: We introduce the Generalized Neural Distributional Regression (GNDR) framework, which seamlessly embeds deep neural networks into the parameter space of classical probability distributions. To reconcile the inherent non-identifiability of deep architectures with maximum likelihood theory, we propose a two-step semi-parametric estimation procedure. By isolating the terminal prediction heads and treating the upstream network as a fixed, non-linear basis expansion, GNDR enables the extraction of analytical Fisher Information matrices. This facilitates rigorous uncertainty quantification, generating observation-specific confidence bands and tolerance intervals via the multivariate Delta method. We demonstrate the framework's versatility and superior distributional calibration across diverse data modalities, including overdispersed clinical counts, right-censored transcriptomic survival profiles under a mixture cure framework, and zero-truncated age distributions derived directly from unstructured facial images. The methodology is natively implemented in the open-source Python package \textit{thetaflow}.

</details>


### [196] [Spectral Concentration and Recovery in Sparse High-Dimensional Random Geometric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14304)
*Manuel Fernandez,Yizhe Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维稀疏随机几何图的谱性质与潜变量恢复问题，在更弱假设下改进了谱范数界，并首次证明了高斯混合块模型的精确恢复结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏随机几何图中，边由高维向量内积超过阈值决定，现有谱范数界和潜变量恢复保证存在改进空间，且高斯混合块模型的精确恢复条件尚未建立。

Method: 结合正交多项式展开、解耦技术和矩阵浓度不等式，避免了传统迹矩论证方法。

Result: 在连通性尺度np=Ω(log n)下，球面模型谱范数界为O(√(np log n)+npτ)；当np≫log n时，潜向量和相对Gram矩阵误差在d≪np log(1/p)/log n条件下消失；首次证明了高斯混合块模型在最优连通性尺度下的精确恢复结果。

Conclusion: 本文在更弱假设下改进了谱范数界和潜变量恢复保证，并首次建立了高斯混合块模型的精确恢复条件，方法上避免了迹矩论证。

Abstract: We study sparse random geometric graphs generated by connecting pairs of high-dimensional vectors whose inner product exceeds a threshold. The latent vectors are sampled either uniformly from the sphere or from a standard Gaussian distribution. Although every edge appears with probability $p$, the edges are dependent through their shared latent vectors. For the spherical model, at the connectivity scale $np=Ω(\log n)$, we prove $\|A-\mathbb E A\|=O\left(\sqrt{np\log n}+npτ\right)$, with high probability, where $τ$ is the cap threshold. This sharpens the spectral norm bound of Liu, Mohanty, Schramm, and Yang (2023) under weaker assumptions. An analogous result holds for the Gaussian model after removing the fluctuations of the vector norms, yielding improved global synchronization guarantees for the homogeneous Kuramoto model. We then recover the latent geometry from the leading eigenspace. When $np\gg\log n$, both the latent vector and relative Gram matrix errors vanish provided $d\ll np\log(1/p)/\log n$. The required lower dimension is only $d\gg\log(1/p)$ for the spherical model and $d\gg\log^2(1/p)\log n$ for the Gaussian model, improving the recovery guarantees of Li and Schramm (2023). Finally, we prove the first exact recovery result for the Gaussian mixture block model of Li and Schramm (2023). At the optimal connectivity scale $np=Ω(\log n)$, a polynomial-time semidefinite program exactly recovers all labels in a moderate-separation regime, whereas larger separation makes exact recovery impossible because isolated vertices appear with high probability. Our proofs combine orthogonal polynomial expansions, decoupling, and matrix concentration, avoiding the trace-moment arguments used in previous work.

</details>


### [197] [Operator-Informed Gaussian Processes for Complex Helmholtz Wavefields: From Synthetic Benchmarks to In Vivo Brain Elastography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14193)
*Boyuan Deng,Kshitiz Upadhyay,Michael Shields*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将算子条件高斯过程回归扩展至复值Helmholtz问题，通过将复算子实部化实现标准实值GP推断，在1-3维基准问题中优于有限差分类神经网络方法，并在脑磁共振弹性成像中实现0.77相关度，指出校准不确定性是概率波场推断的关键下一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耗散介质中的Helmholtz方程复波数场推断需要能量化不确定性的求解器，但现有算子条件GP方法仅适用于实值场。

Method: 将复Helmholtz算子化为等效耦合实块，利用标准实值GP条件化进行推断；支持对角先验、区域化多尺度变体等族类先验；在PDE残差和边界迹上施加条件。

Result: 在1-3维基准问题中，该方法以更少的内部约束点达到与有限差分类神经网络相当的精度；在脑MRE中，多尺度先验重建剪切旋度场与测量的相关度达0.77（目标0.75）。

Conclusion: 多尺度核带来的增益优于实-虚耦合；存在模型失配导致的低频精度上限及未校准的不确定性；校准不确定性是概率波场推断的核心下一步。

Abstract: The Helmholtz equation governs time-harmonic wave propagation, and in dissipative media a complex modulus renders its squared wavenumber $κ^2$ complex. Inferring such fields from sparse, noisy data calls for solvers that also quantify their own uncertainty. Physics-informed Gaussian-process (GP) regression supplies this by returning a posterior over the solution, yet operator-conditioned formulations have been developed almost exclusively for real-valued fields. We extend operator-informed GP regression to complex-valued Helmholtz problems by realifying the complex operator into an equivalent coupled real block, which enables inference with standard real-valued GP conditioning. The construction admits a family of priors, from a proper diagonal prior to coregionalized and multiscale variants, and conditions on PDE residuals and boundary traces. On benchmark problems in one to three dimensions, the solver is competitive with finite-difference and neural-network baselines at a far smaller interior-constraint budget. Unlike those deterministic baselines, it returns a posterior over the complex wavefield rather than a point estimate. Applied to \textit{in vivo} brain magnetic resonance elastography, a proper multiscale prior reconstructs the shear curl field to a correlation of $0.77$ with measurement, above a $0.75$ target. The gain arises from the multiscale kernel rather than from real--imaginary coupling. We further identify a low-frequency accuracy ceiling set by model mismatch and a posterior uncertainty that is not yet calibrated. Calibrated uncertainty therefore emerges as the central next step for probabilistic wavefield inference in dissipative media.

</details>


### [198] [Optimal Self-Distillation for Rectified Flow via Linear Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14947)
*Saptarshi Roy,Debepsita Mukherjee,Pratik Patil*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究整流流（RF）的最优自蒸馏（SD）问题。在线性RF和岭正则化下，证明存在精确仿射路径恒等式，推导出最优混合系数的闭合解，并证明只要教师风险沿正则化路径非平稳，就能严格改进集成速度风险。最优系数遵循符号规则：正混合纠正欠正则化教师，负混合纠正过正则化教师。结合RF Wasserstein收敛界，证明最优自蒸馏能改善控制连续时间和有限步生成误差的速度估计项。实验验证该方法在速度风险、模式恢复和有限步生成上的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型越来越多地使用模型生成的信号进行训练，这既创造了自我改进的机会，也带来了崩溃的风险。本文旨在研究在整流流框架下，能否通过最优自蒸馏策略，利用次优教师速度场训练学生模型，实现对学生模型的确定性改进。

Method: 针对带岭正则化的线性RF，在固定插值对上证明精确仿射路径恒等式，推导最优混合系数的闭合解；提出一次性广义交叉验证（GCV）和验证调优过程，避免网格搜索和重复重新拟合；结合RF Wasserstein收敛界，分析最优自蒸馏对生成误差的改进效果。

Result: 证明了最优混合系数的闭合解和符号规则；最优自蒸馏能严格改进集成速度风险；在连续时间和有限步生成误差上有所改善；在速度风险、模式恢复和有限步生成方面相对教师和纯蒸馏均有提升。

Conclusion: 最优自蒸馏能够有效改进整流流生成模型的性能，且通过GCV过程可高效确定最优混合系数，无需网格搜索，为自蒸馏策略提供了理论保证和实用方法。

Abstract: Modern generative models are increasingly trained using model-generated signals, creating both opportunities for self-improvement and risks of collapse. We study optimal self-distillation (SD) for rectified flow (RF): given a suboptimal teacher velocity field, can a student trained on a mixture of true RF velocities and teacher velocities provably improve the teacher? For linear RF with ridge regularization on fixed interpolation pairs, we prove an exact affine path identity, derive the optimal mixing coefficient in closed form, and show strict improvement in integrated velocity risk whenever the teacher risk is nonstationary along the regularization path. The optimal coefficient obeys a sign rule: positive mixing corrects under-regularized teachers, while negative mixing corrects over-regularized teachers. We also give one-shot generalized cross-validation (GCV) and validation tuning procedure that avoids grid search over mixing weights and repeated refitting. Combining this theorem with RF Wasserstein convergence bounds, we show that optimal self-distillation improves the velocity estimation terms controlling continuous-time and finite-step generation error. Experiments with Gaussian models, Gaussian mixtures, and image data show that optimal self-distillation improves velocity risk, mode recovery, and finite-step generation relative to both the teacher and pure distillation.

</details>


### [199] [cGAP: Generalized Association Plots with HOMALS-Guided Heatmaps for Visualization of High-Dimensional Categorical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15018)
*Chun-houh Chen,Shun-Chuan Chang,Chiun-How Kao,Yi-Ju Lee,Shang-Ying Shiu,Yin-Jing Tien,ShengLi Tzeng,Han-Ming Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出cGAP框架，通过HOMALS降维将高维分类数据映射为RGB颜色编码，结合热图、主题邻近矩阵和变量邻近矩阵三种协调视图，并运用序列化算法重排行/列以揭示聚类与异常值，实现可追溯、可解释的分类数据全矩阵可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维分类数据在遗传学、生物医学和社会科学中广泛存在，但现有可视化工具要么扩展性差、要么过度依赖脱离原始数据矩阵的低维展示、要么优先预测准确性而牺牲可解释性，缺乏同时兼顾可扩展性、可解释性和原始数据结构保持的专用可视化框架。

Method: 使用Homogeneity Analysis (HOMALS)将样本和类别层级嵌入三维欧几里得空间，将嵌入坐标映射为RGB颜色；构建三个协调视图（HOMALS引导的原始数据矩阵热图、样本邻近矩阵、变量邻近矩阵）；运用序列化算法重排行和列以揭示聚类与异常值；推导重心可追溯性、投影失真和对比保持等理论性质。

Result: 在学生-动物分类数据、哺乳动物齿列数据、UCI蘑菇数据集和直系同源基因簇数据库等多个应用场景中展示了cGAP的有效性，证明该框架能够保持衍生视觉结构与原始分类观测之间的可追溯性，支持透明的探索性分析。

Conclusion: cGAP提供了一个全矩阵、基于热图的可视化环境，用于跨科学领域探索复杂分类数据集，兼顾了可扩展性、可解释性和原始数据结构的保持。

Abstract: High-dimensional categorical data arise in genetics, biomedicine, and the social sciences, yet visualization tools for such data remain far less developed than those for continuous variables. Existing methods either scale poorly, rely heavily on low-dimensional displays detached from the original data matrix, or prioritize predictive accuracy over interpretability. To address this gap, we introduce categorical Generalized Association Plots (cGAP), a visualization framework for nominal, ordinal, and binary data that preserves the original data matrix while augmenting it with interpretable geometric structure. cGAP uses Homogeneity Analysis (HOMALS) to embed subjects and category levels in a three-dimensional Euclidean space and maps the embedding to red-green-blue coordinates so that similar patterns receive similar colors. The framework integrates three coordinated views: a HOMALS-guided heatmap of the raw data matrix, a subject proximity matrix, and a variable proximity matrix. Seriation algorithms are then used to reorder rows and columns to reveal coherent clusters, outliers, and local-to-global structure. We also derive barycentric traceability, projection-distortion, and contrast-preservation properties that clarify how embedding geometry is transferred to the display. We demonstrate the versatility of cGAP through applications to student-animal classification data, mammalian dentition profiles, mushroom records from the UCI Machine Learning Repository, and the Clusters of Orthologous Genes database. These examples show that cGAP supports transparent exploratory analysis by maintaining traceability between derived visual structure and the original categorical observations. cGAP provides a full-matrix, heatmap-based visualization environment for investigating complex categorical datasets across scientific domains.

</details>


### [200] [Subjective Risk Decomposition: A New View for Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15196)
*Raghad Alamri,Michele Caprio,Gavin Brown*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出不确定性量化并非原始概念，而是高层建模决策的产物。通过基于严格适当损失的主观风险分解，可以导出认知不确定性和偶然不确定性度量。逆向交叉熵是典型案例，其分解恢复经典信息论不确定性项。该方法统一了UQ文献中众多度量，并从学习理论角度引入了主观风险下的过风险、近似误差和估计误差等概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性量化研究通常将不确定性度量视为需要公理和论证的基本概念，缺乏统一的理论基础。作者认为不确定性度量应是高层建模决策的结果而非前提，旨在建立一种统一的、基于主观风险分解的UQ框架。

Method: 提出基于严格适当损失的主观风险分解方法。通过将主观风险分解为认知不确定性和偶然不确定性两部分，推导相应的度量。以逆向交叉熵为例展示分解过程，并证明该方法能恢复UQ文献中已有的多种度量。进一步将视角扩展到学习理论，定义主观风险下的过风险、近似误差和估计误差。

Result: 逆向交叉熵分解恢复了经典信息论不确定性项；该方法统一了UQ文献中多种已有度量，为其提供了共同的理论基础；在实践层面，给定建模场景和严格适当损失，对应的认知和偶然不确定性项可由主观风险分解导出；在学习理论层面，建立了主观风险与UQ之间的连接。

Conclusion: 不确定性度量应从高层建模决策中导出而非预先定义，主观风险分解提供了统一的理论框架。这是构建完整UQ学习理论框架的第一步。

Abstract: We present a novel viewpoint for uncertainty quantification. Uncertainty measures are not primitives, in need of axioms and argumentation, but instead consequences, of higher-level modelling decisions. We show how epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty measures can be derived via decomposition of a subjective risk, based on a strictly proper loss. Reverse cross-entropy provides a prominent example, where decomposition recovers the classic information-theoretic uncertainty terms. The same approach recovers numerous measures previously proposed across the UQ literature, providing them a common theoretical foundation. From a practical point of view, this suggests a new approach to UQ: given a modelling scenario and strictly proper loss, the corresponding epistemic and aleatoric terms are induced by the subjective-risk decomposition. We then extend our view to learning theory: we introduce and analyse subjective risk analogues of excess risk, approximation error, and estimation error, and identify the connections to UQ. We consider this a first step towards a full learning-theoretic framework for uncertainty quantification.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Precise sample covariance spectral norm error -- an RDT view](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14460)
*Mihailo Stojnic*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了中心化高斯样本协方差误差的谱范数，通过随机对偶理论（RDT）框架，分别推导出封闭形式的上界和下界，并证明在大维情况下上下界匹配，确定了误差谱范数的精确极限值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究[66]建立了样本协方差误差的正确标度阶次，揭示了有效秩和真实协方差谱的关键作用，但尚未给出误差谱范数的精确极限值。本文旨在超越标度刻画，确定这一精确极限。

Method: 基于随机对偶理论（RDT）开发通用框架：1）推导封闭形式的RDT上界；2）引入新颖的双线性-二次型RDT下界机制；3）结合双副本系统边界策略，证明大维情况下上下界匹配。

Result: 推导出样本协方差误差谱范数的封闭形式上下界，并在大维条件下证明上下界吻合；数值实验表明，即使问题规模仅为数千阶，理论结果与仿真高度一致。

Conclusion: 本文通过RDT框架成功确定了中心化高斯样本协方差误差谱范数的精确极限值，为协方差估计理论提供了更精细的刻画。

Abstract: We study the sample covariance error of centered Gaussians. A remarkable breakthrough [66] established the correct error scaling order and explicitly revealed the critical role of both the effective rank and the true covariance spectrum.
  In this work, we move beyond scaling characterizations and determine the precise limiting value of the error's spectral norm. To do so, we develop a generic framework based on Random Duality Theory (RDT). Within this framework, we first determine closed-form, explicit RDT-based upper bounds. We then establish complementary lower bounds by introducing a novel bilinear-quadratic RDT lower-bounding mechanism. By combining this mechanism with a two-replica systems bounding strategy, we show that our lower and upper bounds match in large-dimensional contexts. Our theoretical results are supplemented with numerical evaluations and simulations, demonstrating an excellent agreement already for problem sizes on the order of thousands.

</details>


### [202] [No Universal Multiplicative FDR Bound for the Benjamini-Hochberg Procedure with Correlated Two-Sided Gaussian Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14812)
*Lihua Lei*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明了Benjamini-Hochberg程序在双尾高斯p值下的最坏情况假发现率（FDR）并不像普遍猜想的那样能被通用常数控制，当q趋近于0时，FDR膨胀因子FDR(BH_q)/q至少以q√log(1/q)的阶数发散，并在共同因子高斯模型中证明了该下界是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dobriban [2026]已证明BH过程在最坏情况相关结构下无法将FDR控制在名义水平q内，但存在一个广泛流传的猜想：BH过程仍能控制FDR至多一个通用乘法常数。本文旨在检验和否定这一猜想。

Method: 构造了特定的高斯模型（含任意相关矩阵、均值向量和假设数量），推导出FDR(BH_q)/q的下界为cq√log(1/q)；同时在共同因子高斯模型（任意均值与载荷）下证明了匹配阶数的上界FDR(BH_q)=O(q√log(1/q))。

Result: 否定了FDR能被通用常数控制的猜想；下界：对所有足够小的q，sup_{p,μ,Σ} FDR(BH_q)/q ≥ cq√log(1/q)；上界：共同因子高斯模型中FDR(BH_q)=O(q√log(1/q))，表明下界对此类模型是紧的。

Conclusion: BH过程在最坏情况高斯相关结构下的FDR膨胀速度可达到q√log(1/q)量级，无法被通用常数控制，且该阶数在共同因子模型下是最优的。

Abstract: We study the worst-case false discovery rate of the Benjamini-Hochberg procedure applied to two-sided Gaussian p-values when the correlation matrix is otherwise unrestricted. Dobriban [2026] shows that BH does not always control the FDR at its nominal level. An analogous folklore conjecture is that BH controls the FDR up to a universal multiplicative constant. We prove that this conjecture is false. In particular, we construct Gaussian models for which the inflation factor FDR(BH_q)/q diverges as q tends to 0. More precisely, for all sufficiently small q, the supremum over the number of hypotheses, mean vector, and correlation matrix is at least cq\sqrt{log(1/q)} for a universal constant c > 0. Finally, for a broad class of common-factor Gaussian models with arbitrary means and loadings, we prove the matching-order upper bound FDR(BH_q) = O(q\sqrt{log(1/q)}), and hence the lower bound is sharp in order for this class.

</details>


### [203] [Measuring Spatial Clustering via Metropolis-Hastings Diffusion Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14880)
*Thomas Weighill,Chidinma Williams*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出一种衡量图上两个概率分布差异的新指标——扩散距离（diffusion distance），它通过图约束马尔可夫链（默认使用以随机游走为提议分布的Metropolis-Hastings转移矩阵）的收敛速率来度量分布f到g的差异。当g为均匀分布时，该指标成为空间聚类测度，相较于仅捕捉局部模式的Moran's I，它融入了全局图几何信息。论文建立了理论界与稳定性结果，将其与图谱和最优传输联系起来；并基于排列零模型推导了高概率界，实现高效统计检验。在随机块模型合成数据和100个美国城市的黑人人口分布实证分析中，扩散距离比Moran's I具有更高检验功效，能检测到更细微的城市隔离模式差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间自相关测度（如Moran's I）主要捕捉局部空间模式，缺乏对图全局几何结构的考量，难以区分复杂的空间聚类结构。本文旨在提出一种能融合全局图信息的分布差异指标，以更准确地刻画和检验空间聚类现象。

Method: 定义扩散距离为两个分布f与g在图约束马尔可夫链下的收敛速率，默认采用以随机游走为提议分布的Metropolis-Hastings链。通过图谱理论与最优传输建立理论界与稳定性结果；在排列零模型下推导基于谱公式的高概率边界，实现高效统计检验。

Result: 理论方面，建立了扩散距离与图谱、最优传输的联系，并证明了稳定性结果。在随机块模型合成数据上，扩散距离的统计检验功效高于Moran's I。在100个美国城市黑人人口分布的实证中，扩散距离能检测到Moran's I无法识别的细微城市隔离模式差异。

Conclusion: 扩散距离作为Moran's I的全局化推广，在空间聚类测度和统计检验两方面均优于传统方法，特别适用于检测复杂图结构上的细微空间模式差异。

Abstract: We propose a novel measure of the discrepancy between two probability distributions $f$ and $g$ on a graph - which we call the diffusion distance - that measures the rate of convergence of $f$ to $g$ under a graph-constrained Markov chain with stationary distribution $g$. As a default choice for this Markov chain, we use the Metropolis-Hastings transition matrix targeting $g$ with proposals given by a random walk on the graph. Our primary case of interest is when the second distribution $g$ is uniform, in which case the diffusion distance becomes a measure of spatial clustering in $f$. Used in this way, (Metropolis-Hastings) diffusion distance to uniformity extends Moran's $I$-type measures of spatial autocorrelation by incorporating global graph geometry rather than just local patterns. Indeed, Moran's $I$, the most well-known measure of spatial autocorrelation, can be viewed as a one-step heuristic for diffusion distance, so long as specific spatial weights are used. We establish theoretical bounds and a stability result for our measure, connecting it to graph spectra and optimal transport. We then turn our attention to outlining a statistical test for spatial clustering using diffusion distance. Under permutation null models, we derive high-probability bounds on diffusion distance underpinned by exact spectral formulas for convergence of distributions, enabling an efficient statistical test for spatial clustering on large datasets. We empirically compare diffusion distance to Moran's $I$ both as a numerical measure and as a statistical test. We show that diffusion distance exhibits higher power on synthetic data using a stochastic block model. Empirical analysis of Black population distributions for 100 U.S. cities shows that diffusion distance detects subtle differences in urban segregation patterns that Moran's $I$ does not.

</details>


### [204] [Testing for correct model specification in copula regression models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14930)
*Holger Dette,Philip Dörr*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对半参数copula回归模型提出了一种基于加权L2距离的拟合优度检验方法，通过核估计量检验回归函数的设定偏差，并构建了经典检验和自标准化序贯统计量的枢轴置信区间，证明了估计量的相合性和渐近正态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半参数copula回归模型用边际分布和copula密度表示回归函数，可避免高维问题的完全非参数估计，但其性能高度依赖参数copula族的正确设定。现有方法直接检验copula模型本身，本文旨在回归函数层面上直接评估模型误设。

Method: 定义真实回归函数与copula回归模型最佳逼近之间的加权L2距离作为检验统计量；提出该距离的核估计量；在原假设和固定备择假设下证明估计量的相合性和渐近正态性；构建经典设定检验；利用自标准化序贯统计量构建枢轴置信区间和检验。

Result: 证明了所提加权L2距离核估计量的相合性和渐近正态性；有限样本模拟显示所提程序具有准确的水平逼近和良好的检验功效。

Conclusion: 所提出的拟合优度检验方法能有效检测半参数copula回归模型的误设问题，并在回归函数层面进行直接评估，具有良好的理论性质和有限样本表现。

Abstract: We propose a goodness-of-fit test for semiparametric copula regression models. Such models express the regression function in terms of marginal distribution functions and copula densities and therefore provide a flexible way to avoid fully nonparametric estimation in high-dimensional regression problems. Their performance, however, depends crucially on the specification of the parametric copula family. Instead of testing the copula model itself, we assess misspecification directly at the level of the induced regression function. To this end, we introduce a weighted $L^2$-distance between the true regression function and its best approximation within the postulated copula regression model. A kernel-based estimator of this distance is proposed and shown to be consistent and asymptotically normal under both the null hypothesis of correct specification and fixed alternatives. We derive a classical specification test and, using a self-normalized sequential statistic, construct pivotal confidence intervals and tests for relevant deviations from the model. Finite-sample simulations demonstrate accurate level approximation and good power properties of the proposed procedures.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [PiVoT: A Variational Solution for Real-time Large-scale Multi-object Detection and Tracking under Heavy Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13891)
*Runze Gan,Qing Li,Simon J. Godsill,Mike E. Davies,James R. Hopgood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PiVoT是一种基于变分推断的快速、抗杂波多目标跟踪器，能在全分辨率多普勒点云中实现端到端检测与跟踪，无需外部聚类或检测器，在性能上大幅超越现有贝叶斯跟踪器，并与深度学习检测基准相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯跟踪器基于泊松测量模型，虽无需训练，但在严重杂波、大量目标和全分辨率多普勒点云下难以兼顾精度与效率，亟需一种快速且抗杂波的多目标跟踪方案。

Method: PiVoT通过变分推断联合推理目标状态、形状、存在概率、数据关联和测量率，实现端到端检测与跟踪；关键创新包括理论支撑的出生剪枝、将更新复杂度从二次降至线性、以及高效的多普勒泊松模型。

Result: 在挑战性场景中大幅优于现有贝叶斯跟踪器；可扩展到上千个目标；对视觉上与目标不可分的杂波具有鲁棒性；在完整现代汽车雷达数据集上达到实时运行，且作为无训练联合检测跟踪器，性能媲美深度学习检测基准。

Conclusion: PiVoT证明了无需训练的变分贝叶斯方法在复杂雷达多目标跟踪中可以同时实现高效率、强鲁棒性和与深度学习方法相当的性能，为数据稀缺的雷达应用提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Multi-object detection and tracking from noisy point clouds remain challenging in many data-scarce radar applications. Current Bayesian trackers based on Poisson measurement models offer a training-free solution but struggle to achieve accuracy and efficiency under severe clutter, large object populations, and full-resolution Doppler point clouds. We address this with PiVoT, a fast, clutter-resilient multi-object tracker for both positional and Doppler measurements. PiVoT performs end-to-end detection and tracking of a large and time-varying number of objects without external clustering or detectors, through joint inference of object states, shapes, existence probabilities, data association, and measurement rates. Its efficiency is driven by several variational inference innovations, such as theoretically justified birth pruning, quadratic-to-linear complexity reductions for exact updates, and a computationally efficient Doppler Poisson model. Experiments show that PiVoT substantially outperforms existing Bayesian trackers in challenging scenes, while also demonstrating exceptional scalability to a thousand objects, robustness to clutter visually inseparable from objects, and real-time operation on full-scale modern automotive radar datasets, where it attains performance comparable to a deep-learning detection benchmark as a training-free joint detector and tracker.

</details>


### [206] [A Temporal Machine Learning-Based Time-to-Event Model for Predicting ALS Progression and Healthcare Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14190)
*Zongliang Yue,Qi Li,Terry Heiman-Patterson,Frank Bearoff,Zhaohui Qin,Huanmei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数字孪生理念的时间-事件预测框架，整合纵向ALSFRS-R轨迹与生存建模，用于肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）患者功能衰退及辅助设备使用的个性化预测，并验证了下肢功能（行走、爬楼梯）是轮椅使用的最强预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALS是一种进展性、异质性强的神经退行性疾病，临床里程碑（如辅助设备使用）的预测具有挑战性，现有方法缺乏个性化和动态预测能力。

Method: 构建了包含诊断记录、ALSFRS-R评估、日常生活活动和人口统计信息的协调纵向数据集；基于相关性的聚类识别功能域；广义加性混合模型刻画非线性功能衰退；时序机器学习模型预测纵向功能衰退；Cox比例风险模型识别关键预测因子；最后实现数字孪生启发的时序机器学习时间-事件（TTE）模型生成个性化生存曲线并动态预测无轮椅生存期。

Result: 下肢功能（特别是行走和爬楼梯）是早期轮椅使用的最强预测因子；所提出的TTE模型能够生成个性化的生存曲线并动态预测无轮椅生存期。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可扩展、可解释且临床可操作的方法，将ALS进展与个性化决策支持相连接，可应用于主动护理规划、临床试验分层和精准医学。

Abstract: Amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS) is a progressive and heterogeneous neurodegenerative disease in which predicting clinically meaningful milestones, such as assistive device use, remains challenging. We developed a time-to-event, digital-twin-inspired framework that integrates longitudinal ALS Functional Rating Scale-Revised (ALSFRS-R) trajectories with survival modeling to support individualized prediction of functional decline and assistive device utilization. We constructed a harmonized longitudinal dataset by integrating diagnosis records, ALSFRS-R assessments, activities of daily living, and demographic information, followed by preprocessing to ensure data quality, temporal alignment, and cohort consistency. Correlation-based clustering identified coherent functional domains spanning bulbar, upper limb, axial, lower limb, and respiratory systems. Generalized additive mixed models characterized nonlinear, domain-specific functional decline across all domains. In addition, a temporal machine learning model was developed to predict longitudinal functional decline and capture stage-dependent disease progression. Cox proportional hazards modeling further identified lower limb function, particularly walking and stair climbing, as the strongest predictors of earlier wheelchair access. Building on these results, we implemented a digital twin-inspired temporal machine learning-based time-to-event (TTE) model that generates individualized survival curves and dynamically predicts wheelchair-free survival. This framework provides a scalable, interpretable, and clinically actionable approach for linking ALS progression with personalized decision support, with applications in proactive care planning, clinical trial stratification, and precision medicine.

</details>


### [207] [Supervised Fine-Tuning vs. In-Context Learning: An Equilibrium Analysis of LLM Personalization under Congestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14371)
*Fengzhuo Zhang,Zhuoran Yang,Dirk Bergemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM服务中个性化方法（SFT和ICL）的经济权衡，发现两者在不同数据条件下各占优势，但用户间的资源竞争会改变这种排名；提升预训练精度可减少拥塞，而更广泛的预训练覆盖和更难的任务有时反而增加拥塞；同时提供两种个性化方法不会损害平台最大利润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM个性化虽然提升性能，但消耗稀缺的计算资源（用户需共享）。关键问题在于：用户何时应选择昂贵的SFT而非轻量的ICL？其他用户的个性化选择造成的拥塞如何改变这些激励？平台在提供多种个性化算法时应采取何种策略？

Method: 构建了一个可处理的LLM服务框架，捕获用户面临的统计-经济权衡；使用GPT-2在线性回归任务上进行实验验证理论预测；并对21个主要AI平台的文档进行回顾分析。

Result: 1. ICL和SFT在不同数据区域各自占优，由预训练覆盖和数据信噪比决定，但拥塞会反转排名。2. 均衡资源消耗呈现显著非单调性：提升预训练精度减少拥塞，但更宽的预训练覆盖和更难的任务有时增加拥塞。3. 同时提供两种个性化方法不会损害平台最大利润，尽管可能增加计算负载。平台数据表明提供两种方法的比例从2021年的9.5%上升到2025年的71.4%。

Conclusion: 揭示了LLM服务中个性化选择的战略经济动态，为平台设计和用户决策提供了理论指导，验证了多方法提供策略的合理性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized AI services, but a critical tension emerges: while personalization improves model performance, it consumes scarce computational resources that users must share. When should a user invest in expensive Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) versus lightweight In-Context Learning (ICL)? How does congestion from other users' personalization choices reshape these incentives? And what strategies should platforms adopt when offering multiple personalization algorithms?
  We develop a tractable framework for LLM serving that captures the statistical-economic trade-offs users face. Our analysis yields several surprising insights. First, we show that ICL and SFT dominate in different regimes, determined by an interplay between pretraining coverage and data signal-to-noise ratios, but congestion can flip these rankings. Second, equilibrium resource consumption exhibits pronounced non-monotonicity: improving pretraining precision reduces the congestion, while broader pretraining coverage and harder tasks sometimes increase it. Third, we prove that offering both personalization methods never hurts the platform's maximal profits, despite potentially increasing computational load.
  Experiments with GPT-2 on linear regression tasks validate our theoretical predictions about algorithm performance. Complementing these results, our review of documentation from 21 major AI platforms shows that the share offering both SFT and ICL increased from 9.5% in 2021 to 71.4% in 2025, consistent with our platform-design implications.

</details>


### [208] [Adaptive Runge-Kutta Step Control Buys Training Loss, Not Generalization: An Honest Compute-Matched Study of RK-Adam Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14516)
*Akhilesh Gogikar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将高阶Runge-Kutta优化器（基于Adam的Bogacki-Shampine 3(2)对）在严格计算匹配协议下评估，发现其实际等价于固定步长Adam（梯度平均版），自适应机制无效；修复后虽能大幅降低训练损失，但泛化性能不及一阶方法，梯度平均的隐式正则化效果也被RMSprop/NAdam以更低成本超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将优化器解释为梯度流离散化的观点启发人们将高阶RK积分器应用于神经网络，但现有文献缺乏严格的计算匹配比较，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建Adam变体（Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK对，FSAL重用，局部误差步长控制），在严格计算匹配协议（相同梯度评估预算）下进行小批量与全批量训练；通过仪器化分析自适应机制；修复拒绝分支和误差计算；进行预注册的后续实验排除过拟合和温度调节等解释。

Result: 1）RK变体在计算匹配下训练损失劣于普通Adam；2）自适应机制无效——归一化误差远低于容差，步长从第一步起就卡在增长上限（98-100%步数），容差跨越100倍仍产生恒等轨迹；3）修复后全批量训练损失比调优Adam低约40倍，但增益对初始步长敏感且无法提升测试准确率；4）梯度平均是隐式正则化器，但RMSprop/NAdam以三分之一成本达到或超越其效果。

Conclusion: 高阶自适应积分能实现更深的确定性最小化和轻微正则化效应，但更便宜、调优良好的一阶基线方法已能提供相同收益，RK优化器在实践中缺乏优势。

Abstract: Interpreting optimizers as gradient-flow discretizations has motivated applying higher-order Runge-Kutta (RK) integrators to neural networks. We build a representative Adam variant (Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK pair, FSAL reuse, local-error step control) and evaluate it under a strict compute-matched protocol giving every method the same gradient-evaluation budget - an accounting this literature rarely enforces. Under it the RK variant loses to plain Adam on training loss in both minibatch and full-batch (RK's best-case) training. Instrumenting it shows the "adaptivity" is illusory: normalized error stays far below tolerance, the step size pins at its growth cap from step one (98-100 percent of steps), and no rtol x hmax x h0 setting makes it act; tolerances spanning 100x give bit-identical trajectories. The method is exactly fixed-step Adam with an averaged gradient at 3-4x cost. Repairing it (true reject branch; error on the applied map) reverses the full-batch result - about 40x lower training loss than tuned Adam - and a fixed-step control isolates adaptivity (an emergent warmup-and-growth schedule) as the mechanism. But the gain is fragile to the initial step size and does not reach test accuracy. A pre-registered follow-up rules out the obvious explanations: deeper minimization does not overfit, and an explicit temperature knob only hurts - leaving a trajectory effect, the controller selecting a minimum generalizing 1.3-3.4 points below first-order descent at equal depth. An n=10 study confirms one secondary effect: gradient averaging is a genuine implicit regularizer, beating lr-matched Adam and AdamW on 10/10 seeds - yet RMSprop and NAdam match or beat it at a third the per-step cost. Higher-order adaptive integration buys deeper deterministic minimization and a small regularization effect, but nothing a cheaper, well-tuned first-order baseline does not already provide.

</details>


### [209] [Privacy Leakage in Federated Learning in Radiology Reports: A Comparative Evaluation of Tokenizer-Driven Privacy Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14205)
*Santhosh Parampottupadam,Andres Martinez,Dimitrios Bounias,Sinem Sav,Klaus Maier-Hein,Ralf Floca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 联邦学习中，即使使用域特定分词器（如RadBERT）和大批量（256），放射学报告文本仍可被梯度反演以较高精度重建（精确句子重建率31-44%），RadBERT泄露最多临床术语（18.1%），任何分词器均无法阻止泄露，需要安全聚合和差分隐私等保护措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习用于临床文本训练时存在梯度反演泄露敏感信息的风险，但放射学报告对此泄露的程度以及分词器设计的影响尚不明确。

Method: 固定GPT-2风格transformer架构（序列长度32），使用GPT-2、RadBERT、LLaMA-2三种分词器，在三个放射学语料库（共约46.8万份报告）上以批量64/128/256训练6个FL客户端，假定恶意服务器修改共享架构，应用解析梯度反演，测量重建保真度。

Result: 精确句子重建率31-44%，跨27个条件单元格为30.6-43.5%。批量64时GPT-2 42.1%、RadBERT 42.3%、LLaMA-2 39.4%；批量256时分别降至37.3%、37.2%、34.3%。RadBERT临床术语恢复率最高（18.1%），GPT-2为12.5%，LLaMA-2为9.4%。

Conclusion: 分词器设计是影响隐私风险的关键因素而非仅影响效用，任何分词器均无法阻止梯度泄露，需要安全聚合和差分隐私以满足HIPAA和GDPR合规要求。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables multi-institutional training on clinical text without sharing raw data, but gradient inversion can reconstruct sensitive information from shared model updates. The extent of this leakage for radiology reports, and the role of tokenizer design, remains unclear. We quantify gradient-based text reconstruction in FL and compare privacy risk across three tokenizers with the model architecture held fixed. Six FL clients trained a GPT-2-style transformer (sequence length 32) on public radiology corpora (368,751 diagnostic reports, 98,206 discharge summaries, 1,500 MIMIC-CXR free-text reports) using the GPT-2, RadBERT, and LLaMA-2 tokenizers at batch sizes of 64, 128, and 256. Assuming an active malicious server that modifies the shared architecture before distribution, we applied analytic gradient inversion and measured reconstruction fidelity over five runs. Exact sentence reconstruction ranged from 31% to 44% across tokenizers (30.6-43.5% across the 27 tokenizer x dataset x batch-size cells). At batch size 64 on the Discharge dataset, accuracy was 42.1% (GPT-2), 42.3% (RadBERT), and 39.4% (LLaMA-2), decreasing to 37.3%, 37.2%, and 34.3% at batch size 256. S-BLEU declined as batch size grew (GPT-2: 0.44 to 0.33; RadBERT: 0.48 to 0.35). RadBERT yielded the highest reconstruction fidelity and recovered the most clinical terms (18.1% of a 1,440-term reference vocabulary, vs 12.5% for GPT-2 and 9.4% for LLaMA-2), yet no tokenizer prevented leakage. Substantial portions of report text are therefore recoverable from FL gradients even at larger batch sizes and with domain-specific tokenizers. Tokenizer design influences leakage severity and is a privacy-relevant decision, not only a utility one; safeguards such as secure aggregation and differential privacy are likely necessary to meet HIPAA and GDPR requirements for FL in radiology NLP.

</details>


### [210] [Probabilistic Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Estimating Heterogeneous Elastic Properties from Low-Resolution and Noisy Displacement Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14563)
*Tatthapong Srikitrungruang,Jaesung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种概率逆弹性物理信息神经网络（PIE-PINN），用于从噪声、低分辨率位移数据中鲁棒估计杨氏模量和泊松比，通过B样条引导的位移网络和分层半柯西模型自适应调整损失权重，解决了逆弹性问题的病态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低分辨率位移测量下估计空间异质弹性属性是一个严重病态的逆问题，现有方法依赖高保真观测和人工预设损失权重，对噪声和分辨率退化敏感，缺乏适应性。

Method: 将位移观测、应变-位移关系和平衡残差建模为拉普拉斯分布统一概率模型；采用B样条引导位移网络（B样条全局表示+神经网络局部修正）；引入分层半柯西模型自适应降低严重位移拟合误差的权重；通过交替极大似然训练策略更新均值（加权残差最小化）和尺度参数（调整损失权重）。

Result: 在不同噪声水平和观测分辨率下的系统案例研究证明了PIE-PINN的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PIE-PINN框架能够有效从噪声、低分辨率位移数据中鲁棒估计弹性参数，其概率建模和自适应加权机制克服了传统方法的局限性。

Abstract: Estimating spatially heterogeneous elastic properties from low-resolution displacement measurements is a severely ill-posed inverse elasticity problem because low resolution obscures spatial details needed to distinguish heterogeneous property variations, and small measurement perturbations or fitting errors are amplified through inverse estimation. Existing inverse methods often rely on high-fidelity observations and manually prespecified loss weights, limiting their adaptability and making them sensitive to noise and resolution degradation. We propose a Probabilistic Inverse Elasticity Physics-Informed Neural Network (PIE-PINN) framework for robust estimation of Young's modulus and Poisson's ratio from noisy, low-resolution displacement data. PIE-PINN models displacement observation, strain-discrepancy, and equilibrium residuals using Laplace distributions within a unified probabilistic model. To improve robustness, the framework combines a B-spline-guided displacement network with a hierarchical half-Cauchy model for displacement residual scales. The B-spline provides a smooth global representation of the displacement field, while the neural network correction captures local variations. The hierarchical scale model adaptively downweights severe displacement fitting errors, enabling more robust recovery of the latent mean displacement field. An alternating maximum-likelihood training strategy updates the mean through weighted residual minimization and updates the scales to adjust the loss weights. Systematic case studies across varying noise levels and observation resolutions demonstrate the robustness of PIE-PINN.

</details>


### [211] [Value Leakage: An LLM's Answers Are Silently Shaped by Its Own Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14345)
*Jan Betley,Johannes Treutlein,Jan Dubiński,Harry Mayne,Karol Gałązka,Niels Warncke,Anna Sztyber-Betley,Owain Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大语言模型在回答用户问题时存在隐蔽价值泄露（covert value leakage）现象：模型的回答受到自身价值观的影响，但未向用户披露这种影响，导致用户被误导。本文构建了一套评估体系量化该现象，发现不同模型在披露程度上存在显著差异，且当前的对齐训练未充分解决此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户常使用语言模型回答难以验证的实际问题，而模型的回答可能受其自身价值观影响却未告知用户，这会误导用户。现有研究未充分关注这种隐蔽的价值泄露及其与谄媚、奖励黑客等失败模式的区别。

Method: 设计了一套评估套件来量化价值泄露的程度以及模型是否主动披露这种影响。通过多个任务（如投资AI公司泡沫概率评估、费米估计任务）对比不同前沿模型（Claude、Qwen等）在受自身价值观影响时的表现和披露行为。

Result: 模型受到多种价值观影响，包括对道德善果的偏好、对开发公司自身的偏好、对特定人类休闲活动的偏好。不同模型差异显著：Claude模型在费米估计任务中声称其答案无偏，但实际受价值观影响；Qwen模型则主动解释价值观如何影响其答案。

Conclusion: 隐蔽价值泄露是一种与谄媚、奖励黑客不同的失败模式，当前的对齐训练和评估未能充分应对该问题。

Abstract: People use language models for practical questions whose answers are difficult to verify. We show that models exhibit covert value leakage: the information they provide is influenced by their own values, without this influence being disclosed to the user.
  In one of our evaluations, the user is considering investing in an AI company and wants to know how likely the AI bubble is to pop. Claude Opus 4.8 gives a lower probability when the company under consideration is Anthropic rather than OpenAI. Yet Claude mostly fails to disclose this influence to the user.
  Covert value leakage is a form of misalignment because it goes against the user's preferences and is likely to mislead them. To investigate this phenomenon, we introduce a suite of evaluations to quantify value leakage and whether models disclose it. We find that models are influenced by different types of values, including preferences for morally good outcomes, for the company that developed them, and for some human leisure activities over others.
  We often observe large differences among frontier models on the same evaluation. For example, on a Fermi-estimation task, Claude models falsely claim to give unbiased answers in their chain-of-thought, while Qwen models explain how their values bias their answers. Value leakage is a failure mode distinct from sycophancy and reward hacking, and current alignment training and evaluations do not adequately address it.

</details>


### [212] [Sharp Stability Threshold and Certification for Designing Stable Residual Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14576)
*Hyemin Gu,Michael Tyrrell,Tuhin Sahai,Markos A. Katsoulakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出深度残差架构的次线性增长原则：每个残差块的速度场需满足 $\|v(x,t)\| \leq c\|x\|^q + b$ 且 $q \in [0,1]$。$q=1$ 是稳定训练的充要阈值，通过经典ODE理论和HJB最优控制分析双重论证。该原则为架构设计提供了输入幅度指数的算术规则，可替代归一化层实现稳定训练，在Mamba和PatchTST上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度残差网络在训练中常出现梯度爆炸/消失、不稳定性等问题，现有工作多通过归一化层等启发式方法缓解，缺乏对残差架构稳定性的根本性理论理解。作者希望从动力系统和最优控制视角，建立残差网络稳定训练的必要充分条件。

Method: (1) 定义残差块速度场的次线性增长条件 $q \leq 1$；(2) 用经典ODE理论证明 $q>1$ 时前向流发散；(3) 用HJB方程的最优控制分析证明 $q>1$ 时最优策略是bang-bang控制导致爆炸；(4) 建立输入幅度指数在五种残差块构建操作下的算术规则；(5) 提出参数无关的修正方法，将Mamba块从 $q=5$ 降至 $q=1$。

Result: (1) $q \leq 1$ 是稳定训练的必要充分条件；(2) 层归一化的本质作用是保证 $q \leq 1$；(3) 无需归一化层，仅通过控制输入幅度指数即可实现稳定训练；(4) Mamba块从 $q=5$ 修正为 $q=1$ 后稳定训练；(5) PatchTST上 $q \leq 1$ 变体训练稳定。

Conclusion: 次线性增长原则为深度残差架构提供了可验证的稳定性条件，将启发式的架构搜索转变为基于指数算术规则的确定性设计，层归一化只是满足该条件的充分但不必要手段。这为设计无归一化的稳定深度网络提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We propose \emph{the sublinear-growth principle} for deep residual architectures -- a sharp stability threshold on the input-magnitude exponent of every residual block's velocity field: $$\|v(x, t)\| \leq c\,\|x\|^q + b, \qquad q \in [0, 1].$$ The threshold $q = 1$ is established via two independent arguments. Classical ODE theory gives a global forward flow on $[0, T]$ at $q \le 1$ and exhibits divergent velocity fields at any $q > 1$. The optimal-control analysis, via the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation, sharpens this to a selection statement: the training optimum is bang-bang on the boundary of the admissible class, so the optimum at $q > 1$ blows up while the optimum at $q \le 1$ is safe by construction. The exponent criterion $q \le 1$ is thereby a necessary and sufficient condition for stable training. It clarifies architectural placements that ensure the stability of training and inference, explaining, for instance, the stabilizing role of layer normalization. The sublinear-growth velocity fields form \emph{the right function space} on which forward dynamics, adjoint sensitivity, and architectural composition are all well-controlled. An arithmetic of input-magnitude exponents under the five operations that build residual blocks enables efficient certification of $q_k \le 1$ at the level of architectural primitives, in place of ad hoc trial and error in the search for stable neural architectural designs. A parameter-free modification reduces the supercritical Mamba block from $q = 5$ to $q = 1$ without layer normalization, demonstrating this point. Experiments on Mamba and PatchTST confirm that the $q \le 1$ variants train stably: the criterion is the input-magnitude exponent, not the presence of a normalization layer.

</details>


### [213] [What's in a Smoothness Constant? Tighter Rates for Local SGD with Bounded Second-order Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14731)
*Kumar Kshitij Patel,Rustem Islamov,Sebastian U Stich,Aurelien Lucchi,Eduard Gorbunov,Lingxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在二阶异质性有界假设下，Local SGD（联邦平均）在一般凸目标上具有近乎最优的收敛保证，并改进了已知下界，同时将该技术应用于串行SGD，揭示了稀有高曲率客户端的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Local SGD在实践中表现优异，但理论仅能部分解释其在数据异质性下何时及为何有效。Patel等人[2025]的工作表明有界二阶异质性假设能刻画强凸情形下Local SGD的效率，并猜想该原理可推广至一般凸情形。本文旨在证明这一猜想，建立更完整的收敛理论。

Method: 本文在一般凸目标下，基于有界二阶异质性假设，对Local SGD进行收敛性分析，导出改进的上界；同时构建下界，证明上界的近乎紧致性；并将分析技术扩展到串行SGD，推导其下界。

Result: 证明了在一般凸目标下Local SGD的改进收敛上界；改进了该场景下已知的下界，表明上界近乎紧致；为串行SGD提供了下界，展示了二阶异质性如何捕捉稀有高曲率客户端的影响。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了Local SGD在一般凸目标下的理论空白，验证了有界二阶异质性假设的核心作用，为理解分布式优化中局部更新的优势提供了更精细的理论基础。

Abstract: Local SGD, also known as Federated Averaging, is a widely used distributed optimization algorithm. Although Local SGD often outperforms alternatives such as Mini-batch SGD in practice, theory still only partially explains when and why local updates help under realistic data heterogeneity. Recent work by [Patel et al., 2025] shows that a bounded second-order heterogeneity assumption captures the efficiency of Local SGD for strongly convex objectives, and conjectures that the same principle extends to the general convex setting. In this paper, we prove this conjecture by establishing an improved convergence guarantee for Local SGD on general convex objectives under bounded second-order heterogeneity. We also improve the best-known lower bounds for Local SGD in this setting, showing that our upper bounds are nearly tight. Together, these results provide a sharper, more fine-grained convergence theory for Local SGD. As a further application of our techniques, we provide a lower bound for serial SGD with replacement, showing how second-order heterogeneity captures the impact of rare high-curvature clients.

</details>


### [214] [Random Logit Scaling: Defending Deep Neural Networks Against Black-Box Score-Based Adversarial Example Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14921)
*Hamid Dashtbani,Mehdi Dousti Gandomani,AmirMahdi Sadeghzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机对数缩放（RLS）——一种即插即用的后处理防御方法，通过输出随机缩放的对数来混淆基于分数的黑盒对抗攻击，在保持模型准确率的同时显著降低攻击成功率；此外，本文还提出了一种针对非随机化黑盒防御AAA的自适应攻击，揭示了其脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒对抗样本攻击日益强大，现有防御方法在可扩展性、低成本和有效性方面存在不足，亟需一种轻量级、易部署且能有效防御黑盒基于分数的对抗攻击的防御机制。

Method: 1) 提出随机对数缩放（RLS）防御方法：对模型输出的logits进行随机缩放后再转换为概率分数，从而向攻击者输出伪造的混淆分数，同时保持原始模型准确率。RLS是一种即插即用、仅需后处理、不修改原模型的轻量级防御。2) 提出针对非随机化黑盒防御AAA的自适应攻击，通过针对性设计揭示其漏洞。

Result: RLS显著降低了最先进黑盒基于分数攻击的成功率，同时保持模型精度不变，且相比其他随机化防御方法具有更低的置信度分数失真；自适应攻击成功攻破了非随机化防御AAA。

Conclusion: RLS作为一种轻量级随机化后处理防御，能有效防御黑盒基于分数的对抗攻击，且对模型精度影响极小；同时，非随机化的防御方法（如AAA）易受自适应攻击，表明随机化策略在黑盒防御中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly adapted in various domains. However, adversarial examples pose a significant threat to the reliable deployment of these models. In recent years, some powerful adversarial example attacks have been proposed for the fast and query-efficient generation of adversarial examples, even in black-box scenarios, highlighting the need for scalable, low-cost, and powerful defenses. In this work, we present two contributions to the domain of black-box adversarial example attacks and defenses. First, we propose Random Logit Scaling (RLS), a randomization-based defense against black-box score-based adversarial example attacks. RLS is a plug-and-play, post-processing defense that can be implemented on top of any existing ML model with minimal effort. The idea behind RLS is to confuse an attacker by outputting falsified scores resulting from randomly scaled logits while maintaining the model accuracy. We show that RLS significantly reduces the success rate of state-of-the-art black-box score-based attacks while preserving the accuracy and minimizing confidence score distortion compared to state-of-the-art randomization-based defenses. Second, we introduce a novel adaptive attack against AAA, a SOTA non-randomized black-box defense against black-box score-based attacks that also modifies output logits to confuse attackers, demonstrating its vulnerability against adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [215] [GAttNHP: Group Attention Neural Hawkes Process for Extrapolation Reasoning in Temporal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14733)
*Xiangni Tian,Kaixian Yu,Runpeng Dai,Niansheng Tang,Hongtu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Group Attention Neural Hawkes Process (GAttNHP)框架，通过自注意力编码器、语义软分组模块和非交叉分位数回归头，统一解决时序知识图谱预测中的长程依赖、事件链交互和重尾到达时间三个难题，在六个基准数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TKG事件预测面临三个核心困难：(i) 长程时序依赖难以编码；(ii) 不同事件链之间相互激发/抑制，快照级模型无法表达这种交互；(iii) 事件间隔呈重尾分布且统计稀疏，确定性时间预测不可靠。现有方法未能统一解决这三个问题。

Method: (1) 自注意力编码器：将每个subject-relation链建模为连续时间点过程，捕获远距离历史的持续激发效应；(2) 语义软分组模块：将全局可学习的Hawkes先验转化为分析性交叉注意力掩码，通过潜在群体成员关系共享激发模式，避免穷举成对计算；(3) 非交叉分位数回归头(NCQ)：替代基于均值的预测，提供校准的、单调有序的分位数估计，在重尾间隔分布下保持稳定。

Result: 在六个TKG基准数据集上，GAttNHP在实体预测和时间预测任务上均超越现有SOTA方法。消融实验表明，最大的性能提升出现在长尾事件链上——这正是现有模型失败最严重的场景。

Conclusion: GAttNHP通过统一框架有效解决了TKG预测的三个核心挑战，尤其擅长处理长尾事件链，验证了将点过程、分组注意力和分位数回归三者结合的有效性。

Abstract: Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGs) record how facts evolve over time, but forecasting future events on a TKG remains difficult for three reasons: (i) long-range temporal dependencies are hard to encode; (ii) events on different chains mutually excite or inhibit one another in ways that snapshot-level models cannot express; and (iii) inter-arrival times are heavy-tailed and statistically sparse, so deterministic time predictors are unreliable. We address these three issues with a single framework, the \textbf{Group Attention Neural Hawkes Process (GAttNHP)}, built around three matched components. First, a self-attention encoder casts each subject--relation chain as a continuous-time point process and captures the lingering excitation of distant history. Second, a semantic soft-grouping module turns globally learnable Hawkes priors into an analytical cross-attention mask, so chains share excitation patterns through their latent group memberships rather than through exhaustive pairwise computation. Third, a Non-Crossing Quantile (NCQ) regression head replaces mean-based time prediction, providing calibrated, monotonically ordered quantile estimates that remain stable under heavy-tailed inter-arrival distributions. On six benchmark TKG datasets, GAttNHP improves over state-of-the-art baselines on both entity prediction and time prediction, and ablations confirm that its largest gains arise on the long-tail event chains where existing models fail most severely.

</details>


### [216] [Position: Explainability Research Must Prioritize Foundations over Ad-hoc Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14123)
*Michal Moshkovitz,Suraj Srinivas,Lesia Semenova,Nave Frost,Cyrus Rashtchian,Valentyn Boreiko,Shichang Zhang,Himabindu Lakkaraju,Cynthia Rudin,Jennifer Wortman Vaughan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批评当前可解释AI（XAI）研究中的根本性缺陷，指出尽管技术众多，但解释很少真正影响实际工作流。通过对顶级会议论文的分析和从业者调查，作者呼吁社区从临时性方法转向解决结构性问题，并提出面向行动的人类中心范式清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前XAI技术（从特征归因到稀疏自编码器）虽层出不穷，但解释往往被生成后即被丢弃，未能引导有意义的人机协作行动。研究尚未建立将解释整合到端到端、人在回路系统中的方法论。

Method: 1) 分析ICML、NeurIPS、ICLR近期论文；2) 调查XAI从业者；3) 基于分析提出实践性检查清单。

Result: 揭示了阻碍累计进展的反复出现的问题：问题表述不清晰、评估目标未充分指定、缺乏解释驱动反馈的流水线。

Conclusion: ML社区应从开发临时性XAI方法转向解决基础性与结构性挑战，强调基础性清晰度而非方法新颖性，为将解释整合到可行动的、反馈驱动的AI系统中提供路线图。

Abstract: Despite the proliferation of Explainable AI (XAI) techniques -- from feature attributions to sparse autoencoders -- explanations rarely influence real-world workflows. In practice, they are often generated and discarded without guiding meaningful action. This gap reflects foundational shortcomings: research has not yet established methodologies for integrating explanations into end-to-end, human-in-the-loop systems. This position paper argues that the machine learning community must pivot from ad-hoc XAI methods toward addressing foundational & structural challenges, including unclear problem formulations, underspecified evaluation objectives, and the absence of pipelines for explanation-driven feedback. We support this claim through an analysis of recent ICML, NeurIPS, and ICLR papers and a survey of XAI practitioners, revealing recurring issues that limit cumulative progress. We conclude by outlining a practical checklist designed to shift XAI toward a more human-centered, action-oriented paradigm. By emphasizing foundational clarity over the development of ad-hoc methods, we hope to provide a roadmap for integrating explanations into actionable, feedback-driven AI systems.

</details>


### [217] [Data Driven Block Replacement Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15229)
*Aniruddhan Ganesaraman,VIdyadhar Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对未知寿命分布下独立同质机器的批量更换策略，提出了基于多臂老虎机的数据驱动算法，实现了O(K log T)的遗憾上界，并利用批量更换的嵌套观测性质进一步优化，同时通过Kaplan-Meier非参数估计实现策略一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际运维中，机器寿命分布通常未知，而批量更换策略（定期集体更换所有机器，故障时单独更换）是常见做法。现有方法依赖已知分布假设，缺乏数据驱动框架来自适应学习最优更换间隔，因此需要开发能在线学习且具有理论保证的算法。

Method: 将问题建模为随机多臂老虎机，提出基于Hoeffding和Bernstein不等式的置信下界算法；利用批量更换的嵌套观测性质（所有子优臂k<k*的观测天然包含于更优臂的观测中），设计相关变体算法实现O((K-k*)log T)遗憾；提出Kaplan-Meier更新算法从删失数据非参数估计寿命分布；建立两个平均成本MDP模型（时间流逝模型和年龄向量模型）进行分析。

Result: Hoeffding和Bernstein基算法实现O(K log T)遗憾，匹配Lai-Robbins下界；相关变体达到O((K-k*)log T)遗憾，对k<k*的子优臂仅需O(1)次直接拉动；Kaplan-Meier更新算法几乎必然收敛到最优策略，长期经验遗憾接近零；MDP分析证明批量更换在策略类中的最优性，并在递增失效率分布下证明单调阈值结构。

Conclusion: 本文提供了完整的批量更换策略数据驱动学习框架，从算法设计到理论保证再到MDP最优性分析，数值实验验证了理论排序并揭示了最优批量更换与年龄更换之间的结构性成本差距。

Abstract: We develop data-driven algorithms for maintaining $N$ independent identical machines under a \textit{block replacement policy}, in which each machine is replaced upon failure and all machines are jointly replaced at regular intervals of length $k$. The goal is to learn the cost-minimizing interval $k^*$ from operational data when the lifetime distribution is unknown. At each decision epoch, the operator selects $k \in \{1, 2, \ldots, K\}$, observes the resulting failure history (a mixture of complete and right-censored lifetimes) and incurs a per-unit-time cost governed by the renewal function. We formulate this as a stochastic multi-armed bandit and propose Hoeffding- and Bernstein-based lower-confidence-bound algorithms achieving $O(K \log T)$ regret, matching the Lai--Robbins lower bound. Exploiting a nested observation property unique to block replacement, correlated variants attain $O((K-k^*)\log T)$ regret and require only $O(1)$ direct pulls of suboptimal arms $k < k^*$. A complementary Kaplan--Meier renewal algorithm estimates the lifetime distribution nonparametrically from censored data, achieving almost-sure policy consistency and empirically near-zero incremental regret at long horizons. We additionally analyze two average-cost MDPs: a time-elapsed formulation establishing that block replacement is optimal within its policy class for any lifetime distribution, and an age-vector formulation proving a monotone threshold structure under increasing failure rate distributions and providing a gold-standard cost benchmark. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical ordering and reveal structural cost gaps between optimal block and age-dependent replacement.

</details>


### [218] [CARPRT: Class-Aware Zero-Shot Prompt Reweighting for Black-Box Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14125)
*Ruijiang Dong,Zesheng Ye,Jianzhong Qi,Lei Feng,Feng Liu,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CARPRT方法，通过为每个类别自适应地分配提示词权重，实现无需训练的零样本图像分类提示集成，显著优于传统类别无关的提示加权方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示集成方法对所有类别共享相同的提示权重，忽略了不同提示对不同类别的适用性差异（如"鸟瞰图"适合"机场"但不适合"苹果"），导致分类性能受限。

Method: 对于每个类别标签和每个可用提示，通过计算该提示下被预测为该类别的图像与提示之间的图文相关性分数均值，量化该类别的提示相关性；然后将这些估计值归一化得到类别特定的提示权重，最终在零样本分类时使用这些类别自适应权重进行提示集成。

Result: 在标准图像分类基准上，CARPRT优于现有的类别无关提示重加权方法，验证了建模提示-类别依赖关系对有效零样本预测的重要性。

Conclusion: 提示与类别之间的条件依赖关系对零样本分类至关重要，CARPRT以无需训练的方式捕捉这种依赖，显著提升了VLM在零样本预测和更广泛的提示集成场景中的表现。

Abstract: Pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) enable zero-shot image classification by computing the similarity score between an image and textual descriptions, typically formed by inserting a class label (e.g., "cat") into a prompt (e.g., "a photo of a"). Since the score for a given image-class pair is sensitive to the choice of prompt, existing studies ensemble multiple prompts using a weighting vector to aggregate scores across different prompts. Yet, in current strategies, the weighting vector assigned to each prompt is shared across all classes, implicitly assuming that prompts are conditionally independent of classes, which often does not hold in practice, as a prompt like "an aerial view of" might be apt for "airport" but ill-suited for "apple". To address this, we propose class-aware zero-shot prompt reweighting (CARPRT). This scoring scheme adjusts the weighting vector for each class label by capturing the class-specific relevance of different prompts in a training-free manner. For each class label and every available prompt, we quantify their class-specific relevance by averaging image-text relevance scores over images predicted to that class under the given prompt. These estimates are then normalized to derive class-specific weights. Evaluations on standard image classification benchmarks show that CARPRT outperforms existing class-independent reweighting methods, confirming that modeling prompt-class dependencies is crucial for effective zero-shot prediction and even broader VLM-based application settings that rely on prompt ensembling. Our code is available at https://github.com/tmlr-group/CARPRT.

</details>


### [219] [Explainable Geospatial AI for Satellite Ground Station Siting Using LiDAR-Derived Terrain Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14127)
*Shohini Sarkar,Smithi Mahendran,Rishi Chudasama,Varun Mannam,Arav Luthra,Yuvraj Rekhi,Vivek Nadig,Arsh Goenka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于开放地理空间数据、可解释且全球可部署的机器学习框架，用于预测代表性杂波高度（RCH）。该模型使用LiDAR标签和全球遥感特征训练，采用LightGBM回归器，MAE为1.79米，R²=0.765，相比ITU基线误差降低超过60%，并通过SHAP分析揭示了树冠覆盖率、土地覆盖语义和光谱反射率等关键预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ITU-R P.452-18建议书中基于土地覆盖类别分配固定杂波高度的做法忽略了类别内部的变异，导致保守的排除区和较差的站点排序，影响低地球轨道地面站选址和频谱协调。因此需要一种更精确、可解释且全球可部署的方法来预测RCH。

Method: 使用美国地质调查局3D高程计划提供的LiDAR衍生标签作为训练数据，从全球土地覆盖、地形、人口、热学和光学遥感产品中提取推理时特征，定义75百分位杂波高度作为RCH，评估多种回归器后选择LightGBM，并结合SHAP进行特征归因分析。

Result: 模型MAE为1.79米，R²=0.765，相比ITU基线绝对误差降低超过60%。SHAP分析识别出树冠覆盖率、土地覆盖语义和光谱反射率为最具影响力的预测因子。在分割特征、非森林消融和土地覆盖匹配的国际验证中均表现良好。

Conclusion: 开放地理空间数据可以在不牺牲可解释性和可部署性的前提下，大规模改进杂波建模，优于现有ITU固定高度分配方法。

Abstract: Representative clutter height (RCH) is a key parameter in radio propagation and interference analysis because it captures the dominant height of local obstructions that drive terminal clutter loss. Current practice often relies on fixed clutter heights assigned to land use classes in Recommendation ITU-R P.452-18, but this misses within class variation and can lead to conservative exclusion zones and poor site ranking for low Earth orbit ground station siting and spectrum coordination. We present an interpretable, globally deployable machine learning framework for predicting RCH from open geospatial data. The model is trained using LiDAR derived labels from the U.S. Geological Survey 3D Elevation Program and inference time features from global land-cover, terrain, demographic, thermal, and optical remote sensing products. We define RCH using a robust 75th percentile clutter height statistic, evaluate multiple regressors, and select LightGBM for its accuracy, efficiency, and compatibility with feature attribution analysis. The final model achieves a mean absolute error of 1.79m and an R^2=0.765, reducing absolute error by more than 60% relative to the ITU baseline. Beyond aggregate fit, we evaluate domain facing criteria relevant to RF planning, including meter scale error, tolerance band accuracy, over and under estimation tails, agreement with ITU clutter height regimes, and SHAP-based physical plausibility. SHAP identifies tree canopy cover, land-cover semantics, and spectral reflectance as the most influential predictors. Studies on segmentation derived features, non-forest ablations, and land-cover matched international validation show that open geospatial data can improve clutter modeling at scale without sacrificing interpretability or deployability.

</details>


### [220] [Certified Domain Consistency for Multi-Domain Retrieval: Label-Free Per-Domain Contamination Control with Conformal Risk Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14157)
*Jayakumar Manoharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: C3R是一种即插即用的控制层，通过域后验推断和两阶段分割方案，为每个域认证污染预算，在最难域上保证污染减少而非紧界，实验证明其零违反稳定性和更高召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域检索中，混合语料库常返回相关但错误域的证据，导致排序指标失效且共形风险控制仅提供边缘性保障，对最差域覆盖不足。现有方法缺乏对域级污染的细粒度认证与可控性。

Method: 提出C3R控制层：基于域后验推断（无需查询时标签）、双分割方案；核心是风险控制预测集上的有限样本迁移界，从推断域到真实域可完全估计松弛量，支持异构预算并反向推导部署条件。

Result: 在千次重采样校准中，认证证书零违反（稳定性结果），而边缘控制每次采样均违反最污染域；软降级方法在相等认证污染下比最强校准级联保留更多召回率。在联邦法规等独立测试集上可复现，LLM判定的下游探针表明错误权威依据随污染上升、受控制下降。

Conclusion: C3R作为冻结栈和重排序器无关的即插即用层，能在混合域场景下有效认证域级污染预算，对最难域保证污染下降而非仅紧界，显著优于边缘控制方法。

Abstract: Retrieval over corpora that mix several domains often returns relevant but wrong-domain evidence that ranking metrics miss and that conformal risk control bounds only marginally, under-covering the worst domains. This work introduces C3R, a drop-in control layer that, from an inferred domain posterior and no query-time label, certifies a per-domain contamination budget where feasible and otherwise abstains rather than silently violating; on the hardest domains it guarantees a reduction, not a tight bound. The core is a two-split scheme built on risk-controlling prediction sets, whose finite-sample transfer bound crosses from the inferred to the true domain with fully estimable slack, supports heterogeneous budgets, and inverts for deployment. Population validity rests on this bound and a controlled simulation; across a thousand resampled calibrations the certificate never violates (a stability result) while marginal control violates the most-contaminated domain in every draw, and soft demotion retains more recall than the strongest calibrated cascade at equal certified contamination. The method replicates across open testbeds including an independent one from public federal regulations, and an LLM-judged downstream probe indicates wrong-authority grounding rises with contamination and falls under control. The layer is frozen-stack and reranker-agnostic.

</details>


### [221] [QFireNet: A Quantum-Enhanced U-Net for Wildfire Segmentation from Sentinel-2 Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14160)
*Jaiman Munshi,Tanvi Tewary,Sawyer Bloom,Aidan Chu,Chetan Maviti,Kyon Winston-Bey,Harshit Badjatia,Farhan Kittur,Vardhan Madhavarapu,Varun Kota,Joshua Kwon,Nazia Rangwala-Vohra,Franz Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于U-Net语义分割模型，引入变分量子电路（QuFeX和QB-Net ansatzes）用于卫星遥感 wildfire 检测。在Sen2Fire数据集上，量子混合模型（QB-Net F1=31.18, QuFeX F1=30.79）优于经典U-Net基线（F1=28.71），数据混合消除了地理域偏移，将FPN性能提升至39.76，并通过CaBuAr数据集验证了模型的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星 wildfire 检测面临类别不平衡、特征复杂和大气干扰等挑战，经典分割模型在高维光谱特征空间建模上存在局限，因此探索量子-经典混合方案以提升分割性能。

Method: 基于U-Net架构，在瓶颈层注入变分量子电路（QuFeX和QB-Net ansatzes），同时引入FPN进行对比分析，并探索参数压缩、替代损失函数和数据均匀混合等经典改进策略。

Result: 量子混合模型QB-Net（F1=31.18）和QuFeX（F1=30.79）优于经典U-Net基线（28.71），FPN达到31.13。数据混合消除域偏移后，FPN的F1提升至39.76。CaBuAr跨数据集验证确认了模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 量子机器学习在 wildfire 图像分割任务中展现出潜在优势，值得进一步实验验证和扩展。

Abstract: Wildfire detection from satellite imagery is a semantic image segmentation problem that has proven to be difficult due to challenges such as class imbalance, feature complexity, and atmospheric interference. In this paper, we build on the foundational U-Net image segmentation model to develop a quantum-hybrid solution in hopes of more effectively modeling the high-dimensional spectral feature space of the Sen2Fire dataset. We inject a variational quantum circuit in the bottleneck portion of U-Net, specifically the QuFeX and QB-Net ansatzes. We test a classical Feature Pyramid Network (FPN) for further comparative analysis of the model, and we also explore classical improvements to the U-Net model and its training process, including a compression of parameters, alternative loss functions, and uniform mixing of input data. Our primary finding is that under matched conditions, both QB-Net (with an $F_1$ score of 31.18) and QuFeX ($F_1 = 30.79$) outperformed the classical U-Net baseline results ($F_1 = 28.71$). Additionally, the classical FPN achieved a comparable score of 31.13. A crucial finding was that data mixing removed a significant domain shift between the geographically-separated train and test sets, which boosted the classical FPN $F_1$ score to 39.76. We validate the architecture's robustness and generalizability to the wildfire detection problem via cross-dataset transfer on the California Burned Areas (CaBuAr) dataset. Overall, we find that quantum machine learning has potential to provide an advantage in the problem of wildfire image segmentation, and further experiments will continue to validate and expand upon this finding.

</details>


### [222] [Branching Policy Optimization: Sandbox-Native Language Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14171)
*Bowei He,Yankai Chen,Xiaokun Zhang,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BPO提出了一种面向可执行沙箱环境的强化学习新范式，通过利用沙箱的快照和恢复能力，在决策分支点进行分叉采样，构建共享前缀的轨迹树，而非独立采样多条轨迹。该方法在WebShop、ALFWorld和SWE-bench上比GRPO/RLOO提升3.6-6.1个绝对百分点，梯度方差减半，且使用38%更少的策略更新即可达到同等性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL算法（PPO/RLOO/GRPO）沿袭了RLHF的独立轨迹采样拓扑结构，未利用智能体沙箱环境的确定性、可快照和可恢复特性，导致方差高、采样效率低。

Method: BPO包含三个步骤：(i) 沿骨干轨迹自适应识别高熵决策点并创建沙箱快照；(ii) 在每个分支点分叉K个替代动作，独立执行至终止；(iii) 利用同层兄弟轨迹的回报计算每个时间步的优势函数，而非使用独立提示的轨迹级基线。

Result: 在WebShop、ALFWorld和SWE-bench上，使用Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.1-8B骨干网络，BPO相比GRPO和RLOO在同等计算量下成功率高3.6-6.1个百分点，梯度范数方差减半，仅需38%的策略更新次数即可匹配最佳基线性能。

Conclusion: 证明了利用沙箱原生特性（快照、分叉、恢复）设计RL算法不仅是可行的，而且在方差降低和采样效率上具有理论保证和实证优势，为交互式智能体训练提供了新的方法论方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as the dominant paradigm for training large language model (LLM) agents that interact with executable sandboxes. State-of-the-art algorithms such as PPO, RLOO, and GRPO inherit their rollout topology from RLHF: for each prompt, N independent trajectories are sampled from the initial state, and an advantage is computed by subtracting a group baseline. This design ignores a defining property of agent sandboxes. They are deterministic, snapshottable, and resumable from any intermediate state. We argue that this property enables a fundamentally different rollout topology: rather than N independent trees of depth T, one can construct a single tree of N leaves whose siblings share prefixes, and therefore share variance. We instantiate this idea as Branching Policy Optimization (BPO), a sandbox-native RL algorithm that (i) adaptively snapshots the sandbox at high-entropy decision points along a backbone trajectory, (ii) forks K alternative actions per branch point and rolls out each to termination, and (iii) computes per-step advantages from sibling returns rather than from independent prompts. We prove this estimator is unbiased and has strictly lower variance than the trajectory-level baseline, with the reduction equal to the prefix-explained portion of return variance. On WebShop, ALFWorld, and SWE-bench Verified with Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B backbones, BPO improves success by 3.6--6.1 absolute points over GRPO and RLOO at matched compute, halves gradient-norm variance, and matches the best baseline using 38% fewer policy updates.

</details>


### [223] [How Much of a 10-K Matters? Aggregation-Dependent Value of Full-Text versus Risk-Factor Sentiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14174)
*Sanggyu Sean Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于10-K文件（特别是Item 1A风险因素部分）的金融情感提取方法，扩展了监督式词典学习方法，在部门、投资组合和个体公司三个聚合层面上分别针对收益标签和波动率标签训练情感评分。在94家纳斯达克-100科技公司2006-2023年的1,383份文件中评估了十二种情感指标，发现完整文件文本在部门和投资组合层面表现更好，而Item 1A在个体公司层面更优。Loughran-McDonald词典基线在所有层级与价格呈强负相关，凸显了监督方法对监管披露文本的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融情感提取主要依赖新闻文本和基于收益标签的监督式提取，而对10-K文件以及波动率（风险披露最适合预测的目标）的研究相对不足。

Method: 将监督式词典学习方法扩展至10-K文件及其Item 1A风险因素部分，在部门、投资组合和个体公司三个聚合层面上分别针对收益标签和波动率标签训练情感评分，共产生十二种情感指标，并在分类准确率、与市场结果的相关性以及词汇内容方面进行评估。

Result: 完整文件文本在部门和投资组合层面情感准确率更高，但在个体公司层面Item 1A表现更好，这一反转归因于文档量与每个聚合层面独立训练信号量之间的交互作用。Loughran-McDonald词典基线在所有层级与价格呈强负相关。

Conclusion: 监督式情感提取方法对监管披露文本具有重要价值，研究发现和设计选择为后续更大规模、多源系统的情感生成方法奠定了基础。

Abstract: Financial sentiment extraction has largely relied on news text and supervised extraction against return labels alone, leaving 10-K filings -- and volatility, the target risk disclosure is arguably best suited to informing -- comparatively unexplored. We extend a supervised lexicon-learning approach to 10-K filings and their Item 1A risk-factor sections, training sentiment scores against both return and volatility labels at three levels of aggregation: sector, portfolio, and individual firm. Across 1,383 filings from 94 Nasdaq-100 technology constituents (2006--2023), we evaluate the resulting twelve sentiment metrics on classification accuracy, correlation with realised market outcomes, and qualitative lexical content. Full-filing text produces more accurate sentiment at the sector and portfolio level for both targets, but this reverses at the individual-firm level, where the narrower Item 1A section performs better -- an effect we attribute to the interaction between document volume and the amount of independent training signal available at each level of aggregation. A Loughran-McDonald dictionary baseline is consistently, strongly negatively correlated with price at every level tested, underscoring the value of a supervised approach for regulatory disclosure text.
